"For the martyr (shahid) with God [are] six traits: he is forgiven at the first gush [of his blood]; and he sees his seat in the Garden; and he is protected from the punishment of the grave; and he is safe from the greatest terror; and there is placed on his head the crown of dignity, a ruby of which is better than this world and what is in it; and he is married to seventy-two wives of the white-eyed, wide-eyed [ones] (hur 'in); and he is made intercessor for seventy of his relatives."

The list has seven items under "six traits". Ibn Majah 2799, the same report, has "the robe of faith" for the crown, and no number of hur (al-Albani Sahih, Zai isnad Hasan). Neither version is in Bukhari or Muslim. Bukhari 2829 and Muslim 1914 name five kinds of martyr; the Sunan name seven besides killing:

**Abu Dawud 3111** (from Jabir b. 'Atik; also malik:555; nasai:1846; ibnmajah:2803; bukhari:2829; muslim:1914; grade: Al-Albani: Sahih; Muhammad Muhyi Al-Din Abdul Hamid: Sahih; Shuaib Al Arnaut: Hasan; Zubair Ali Zai: Isnaad Hasan)

> إِنَّ اللَّهَ عَزَّ وَجَلَّ قَدْ أَوْقَعَ أَجْرَهُ عَلَى قَدْرِ نِيَّتِهِ وَمَا تَعُدُّونَ الشَّهَادَةَ ‏"‏ ‏.‏ قَالُوا الْقَتْلَ فِي سَبِيلِ اللَّهِ ‏.‏ قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏"‏ الشَّهَادَةُ سَبْعٌ سِوَى الْقَتْلِ فِي سَبِيلِ اللَّهِ الْمَطْعُونُ شَهِيدٌ وَالْغَرِقُ شَهِيدٌ وَصَاحِبُ ذَاتِ الْجَنْبِ شَهِيدٌ وَالْمَبْطُونُ شَهِيدٌ وَصَاحِبُ الْحَرِيقِ شَهِيدٌ وَالَّذِي يَمُوتُ تَحْتَ الْهَدْمِ شَهِيدٌ وَالْمَرْأَةُ تَمُوتُ بِجُمْعٍ شَهِيدٌ

"God, mighty and majestic, has made his reward fall according to his intention. And what do you count as martyrdom?" They said, "Being killed in the way of God." The Messenger of God said: "Martyrdom is seven besides being killed in the way of God: the one dead of plague is a martyr, and the drowned is a martyr, and the one with pleurisy is a martyr, and the one dead of the belly is a martyr, and the one burned is a martyr, and the one who dies under a collapse is a martyr, and the woman who dies with [child], a whole, is a martyr."

Also Nasa'i 1846 (Sahih) and Muwatta 555 (al-Hilali Sahih li-ghayrihi). Bukhari 2480 and Muslim 141 have the martyr killed defending his property. Sa'id b. Zayd's version adds family, blood and religion:

**Abu Dawud 4772** (from Sa'id b. Zayd; also tirmidhi:1421; nasai:4095; ibnmajah:2580; bukhari:2480; muslim:141; grade: Al-Albani: Sahih; Muhammad Muhyi Al-Din Abdul Hamid: Sahih; Zubair Ali Zai: Isnaad Sahih)

> مَنْ قُتِلَ دُونَ مَالِهِ فَهُوَ شَهِيدٌ، وَمَنْ قُتِلَ دُونَ أَهْلِهِ أَوْ دُونَ دَمِهِ أَوْ دُونَ دِينِهِ فَهُوَ شَهِيدٌ

"Whoever is killed defending his property is a martyr; and whoever is killed defending his family, or defending his blood, or defending his religion is a martyr."

- **The pinch.** The martyr "does not find of the touch of killing except as one of you finds of the touch of a pinch (qarsa)" (Tirmidhi 1668; al-Albani Hasan Sahih, Zai Da'if).
- **Qazvin.** Ibn Majah 2780 promises a ruby dome "with seventy thousand door-leaves of gold, at every door-leaf a wife" for forty days' guard at Qazvin. It is fabricated (al-Albani, 'Abd al-Baqi and al-Arna'ut Mawdu').

**3. Remembrance and mosques** (remembrance 55 / 31; mosques 14 / 9). The house for building a mosque is in Bukhari 450 and Muslim 533.
- **A palm.** For one formula, "a palm is planted for him in the Garden" (Tirmidhi 3464; al-Albani Sahih, Zai Da'if). It is not in Bukhari or Muslim.
- **The Scale.** The glorifications after prayer weigh "a thousand five hundred in the Scale" (Abu Dawud 5065; al-Albani Sahih, Zai isnad Hasan). Muslim 2727 has the bedtime formula without the Garden.

**4. Named persons** (51 / 36). Neither of these is in Bukhari or Muslim:
- the ten (Abu Dawud 4649; al-Albani Sahih, Zai isnad Hasan);
- al-Hasan and al-Husayn as masters of the Garden's youth (Tirmidhi 3768; Shakir, al-Albani and Zai Sahih).

**5. Testimony and last words** (50 / 32).

**Abu Dawud 3116** (from Mu'adh b. Jabal; also muslim:26; grade: Al-Albani: Sahih; Muhammad Muhyi Al-Din Abdul Hamid: Sahih; Shuaib Al Arnaut: Hasan; Zubair Ali Zai: Isnaad Hasan)

> مَنْ كَانَ آخِرُ كَلاَمِهِ لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ اللَّهُ دَخَلَ الْجَنَّةَ

"Whoever's last speech is 'There is no god but God' enters the Garden."

Muslim 26 has whoever dies knowing there is no god but God. The last-words form is not in either collection.

**6. Social deeds** (36 / 21). Tirmidhi 2485 reads: [The first thing he said on reaching Madina:] "O people, spread the [greeting of] peace, and feed food, and pray while people are asleep: you will enter the Garden in peace." (Shakir and al-Albani Sahih, Zai isnad Sahih; also Ibn Majah 1334). It is not in Bukhari or Muslim.

**7. Character, speech and humility** (character 16 / 7; speech 15 / 10; humility 12 / 5).

**Tirmidhi 2004** (from Abu Hurayra; also ibnmajah:4246; grade: Ahmad Muhammad Shakir: Hasan Isnaad; Al-Albani: Hasan Isnaad; Bashar Awad Maarouf: Hasan; Zubair Ali Zai: Isnaad Sahih; Tirmidhi: هَذَا حَدِيثٌ صَحِيحٌ غَرِيبٌ "this is a hadith sahih gharib")

> سُئِلَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم عَنْ أَكْثَرِ مَا يُدْخِلُ النَّاسَ الْجَنَّةَ فَقَالَ ‏"‏ تَقْوَى اللَّهِ وَحُسْنُ الْخُلُقِ ‏"‏ ‏.‏ وَسُئِلَ عَنْ أَكْثَرِ مَا يُدْخِلُ النَّاسَ النَّارَ فَقَالَ ‏"‏ الْفَمُ وَالْفَرْجُ

The Messenger of God was asked about what most admits people to the Garden, and he said: "Fear of God (taqwa) and good character." And he was asked about what most admits people to the Fire, and he said: "The mouth and the private part."

"Nothing [is] heavier in the Scale of the believer on the Day of Resurrection than good character" (Tirmidhi 2002; Shakir, al-Albani and Zai Sahih).

**Abu Dawud 4800** (from Abu Umama al-Bahili; grade: Al-Albani: Hasan; Muhammad Muhyi Al-Din Abdul Hamid: Hasan; Zubair Ali Zai: Isnaad Hasan)

> أَنَا زَعِيمٌ بِبَيْتٍ فِي رَبَضِ الْجَنَّةِ لِمَنْ تَرَكَ الْمِرَاءَ وَإِنْ كَانَ مُحِقًّا وَبِبَيْتٍ فِي وَسَطِ الْجَنَّةِ لِمَنْ تَرَكَ الْكَذِبَ وَإِنْ كَانَ مَازِحًا وَبِبَيْتٍ فِي أَعْلَى الْجَنَّةِ لِمَنْ حَسَّنَ خُلُقَهُ

"I am a guarantor of a house in the outskirts (rabad) of the Garden for whoever leaves disputing, even if he is in the right; and of a house in the middle of the Garden for whoever leaves lying, even if he is joking; and of a house in the highest [part] of the Garden for whoever makes his character good."

Swallowing anger is rewarded "until God lets him choose of the hur whichever he wills" (Abu Dawud 4777; al-Albani Hasan, Zai isnad Hasan).

**8. Daughters, orphans and children** (daughters and orphans 15 / 11; children 14 / 13). The miscarried child "drags its mother by its navel cord to the Garden, if she sought reward [from God] for it" (Ibn Majah 1609; al-Albani Sahih, al-Arna'ut and Zai Da'if).

**9. Parents and mothers** (8 / 5). Bukhari 3004 has only the man sent back to his parents. The rest is not in Bukhari or Muslim.

**Nasa'i 3104** (from Mu'awiya b. Jahima al-Sulami (reporting his father Jahima); also ibnmajah:2781; ~bukhari:3004; grade: Abu Ghuddah: Hasan Sahih; Al-Albani: Hasan Sahih; Zubair Ali Zai: Isnaad Sahih)

> أَنَّ جَاهِمَةَ، جَاءَ إِلَى النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَقَالَ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ أَرَدْتُ أَنْ أَغْزُوَ وَقَدْ جِئْتُ أَسْتَشِيرُكَ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ ‏"‏ هَلْ لَكَ مِنْ أُمٍّ ‏"‏ ‏.‏ قَالَ نَعَمْ ‏.‏ قَالَ ‏"‏ فَالْزَمْهَا فَإِنَّ الْجَنَّةَ تَحْتَ رِجْلَيْهَا

Jahima came to the Prophet and said: "O Messenger of God, I wanted to go raiding, and I have come to consult you." He said: "Do you have a mother?" He said: "Yes." He said: "Then keep to her, for the Garden is beneath her two feet."

Also Ibn Majah 2781 (al-Albani Sahih).
- **The middle gate.** "The parent (al-walid) is the middle of the gates of the Garden" (Tirmidhi 1900; Shakir and al-Albani Sahih, Zai isnad Hasan).
- **Garden and Fire.** Of parents: "They are your Garden and your Fire" (Ibn Majah 3662; weak, Da'if by all four graders).

**10. Wives** (4 / 3). None of these is in Bukhari or Muslim.

**Tirmidhi 1161** (from Umm Salama; also ibnmajah:1854; grade: Ahmad Muhammad Shakir: Daif; Al-Albani: Daif; Bashar Awad Maarouf: Hasan; Zubair Ali Zai: Isnaad Hasan; Tirmidhi: هَذَا حَدِيثٌ حَسَنٌ غَرِيبٌ "this is a hadith hasan gharib")

> أَيُّمَا امْرَأَةٍ مَاتَتْ وَزَوْجُهَا عَنْهَا رَاضٍ دَخَلَتِ الْجَنَّةَ

"Any woman [who] died while her husband [was] pleased with her entered the Garden."

Also Ibn Majah 1854 (al-Albani Da'if, Zai Hasan).
- **Mothers who pray.** Ibn Majah 2013: "were it not for what they bring upon their husbands, those of them who pray would enter the Garden" (weak, Da'if by all four graders).
- **The hur wife.** In Tirmidhi 1174 the husband's wife of the hur 'in tells a wife who harms him: "He is only a guest (dakhil) with you; soon he will leave you for us" (Shakir and al-Albani Sahih, Zai isnad Hasan).

**11. Others** (repentance 11 / 8; the community 7 / 6). "Whoever wants the midst (buhbuha) of the Garden, let him hold fast to the community" (Tirmidhi 2165; Shakir, al-Albani and Zai Sahih).

#### What bars the Garden

A bar takes three forms: the person "does not enter the Garden"; he will not smell its scent; or he enters the Fire. The largest groups are killing (27 / 10), wealth (23 / 16), pride (18 / 9) and women (16 / 10).

**Killing.**

**Tirmidhi 1403** (from Abu Hurayra; also ibnmajah:2687; bukhari:3166; grade: Ahmad Muhammad Shakir: Sahih; Al-Albani: Sahih; Bashar Awad Maarouf: Hasan Sahih; Zubair Ali Zai: Sahih; Tirmidhi: حَدِيثُ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ حَدِيثٌ حَسَنٌ صَحِيحٌ "the hadith of Abu Hurayra is a hadith hasan sahih")

> أَلاَ مَنْ قَتَلَ نَفْسًا مُعَاهِدَةً لَهُ ذِمَّةُ اللَّهِ وَذِمَّةُ رَسُولِهِ فَقَدْ أَخْفَرَ بِذِمَّةِ اللَّهِ فَلاَ يَرَحْ رَائِحَةَ الْجَنَّةِ وَإِنَّ رِيحَهَا لَيُوجَدُ مِنْ مَسِيرَةِ سَبْعِينَ خَرِيفًا

"Lo! Whoever kills a soul under treaty (mu'ahada), for whom [is] the protection of God and the protection of His Messenger, has broken the protection of God; so he will not smell the scent of the Garden, and indeed its scent is found from a journey of seventy autumns."

Bukhari 3166 has forty years and no clause on protection. Abu Dawud 2760 has "God has forbidden him the Garden" (al-Albani Sahih, Zai isnad Sahih).

**Wealth and pride.**
- **Free of three.** "Whoever, the spirit leaving the body, is free of three enters the Garden: of pride, and theft from the spoils (ghulul), and debt" (Ibn Majah 2412; al-Albani Sahih, Zai Da'if; also Tirmidhi 1572, Sahih).
- **The toll.** "The man of the toll (sahib maks) does not enter the Garden" (Abu Dawud 2937; weak, Da'if by all three graders).
- **The mustard seed of pride** (Abu Dawud 4091, Tirmidhi 1998) is Muslim 91.
- **The divine saying on pride.** In the Sunan it ends "I hurl him into the Fire" (Abu Dawud 4090; al-Albani and Zai Sahih). Muslim 2620 has the punishment without the Fire.

**Women.**

**Abu Dawud 2226** (from Thawban; also tirmidhi:1187; ibnmajah:2055; grade: Al-Albani: Sahih; Muhammad Muhyi Al-Din Abdul Hamid: Sahih; Zubair Ali Zai: Isnaad Sahih)

> أَيُّمَا امْرَأَةٍ سَأَلَتْ زَوْجَهَا طَلاَقًا فِي غَيْرِ مَا بَأْسٍ فَحَرَامٌ عَلَيْهَا رَائِحَةُ الْجَنَّةِ

"Any woman who asked her husband for divorce without any harm, the scent of the Garden is forbidden to her."

Also Tirmidhi 1187 and Ibn Majah 2055 (Sahih). "The women who seek release (al-mukhtali'at) [from marriage], they are the hypocrites" (Tirmidhi 1186; Shakir, al-Albani and Zai Sahih).

**The reminder of gifts, the undutiful, the drinker and the dayyuth.** None of these is in Bukhari or Muslim.

**Nasa'i 5672** (from 'Abd Allah b. 'Amr; also nasai:2562; grade: Abu Ghuddah: Sahih; Al-Albani: Sahih; Zubair Ali Zai: Hasan)

> لاَ يَدْخُلُ الْجَنَّةَ مَنَّانٌ وَلاَ عَاقٌّ وَلاَ مُدْمِنُ خَمْرٍ

There does not enter the Garden one who reminds [of his gifts] (mannan), nor one undutiful [to parents] ('aqq), nor one addicted to wine (mudmin khamr).

**Nasa'i 2562** (from 'Abd Allah b. 'Umar (via his son Salim); grade: Abu Ghuddah: Hasan Sahih; Al-Albani: Hasan Sahih; Zubair Ali Zai: Isnaad Hasan)

> ثَلاَثَةٌ لاَ يَنْظُرُ اللَّهُ عَزَّ وَجَلَّ إِلَيْهِمْ يَوْمَ الْقِيَامَةِ الْعَاقُّ لِوَالِدَيْهِ وَالْمَرْأَةُ الْمُتَرَجِّلَةُ وَالدَّيُّوثُ وَثَلاَثَةٌ لاَ يَدْخُلُونَ الْجَنَّةَ الْعَاقُّ لِوَالِدَيْهِ وَالْمُدْمِنُ عَلَى الْخَمْرِ وَالْمَنَّانُ بِمَا أَعْطَى

Three, God will not look at them on the Day of Resurrection: the one undutiful to his parents, and the woman who makes herself like men (al-mutarajjila), and the dayyuth. And three will not enter the Garden: the one undutiful to his parents, and the one addicted to wine, and the one who reminds of what he gave (al-mannan).

**Ibn Majah 3377** (from 'Abd Allah b. 'Amr; grade: Al-Albani: Sahih; Muhammad Fouad Abd al-Baqi: Sahih; Shuaib Al Arnaut: Sahih; Zubair Ali Zai: Isnaad Sahih)

> مَنْ شَرِبَ الْخَمْرَ وَسَكِرَ لَمْ تُقْبَلْ لَهُ صَلاَةٌ أَرْبَعِينَ صَبَاحًا وَإِنْ مَاتَ دَخَلَ النَّارَ فَإِنْ تَابَ تَابَ اللَّهُ عَلَيْهِ وَإِنْ عَادَ فَشَرِبَ فَسَكِرَ لَمْ تُقْبَلْ لَهُ صَلاَةٌ أَرْبَعِينَ صَبَاحًا فَإِنْ مَاتَ دَخَلَ النَّارَ فَإِنْ تَابَ تَابَ اللَّهُ عَلَيْهِ وَإِنْ عَادَ فَشَرِبَ فَسَكِرَ لَمْ تُقْبَلْ لَهُ صَلاَةٌ أَرْبَعِينَ صَبَاحًا فَإِنْ مَاتَ دَخَلَ النَّارَ فَإِنْ تَابَ تَابَ اللَّهُ عَلَيْهِ وَإِنْ عَادَ كَانَ حَقًّا عَلَى اللَّهِ أَنْ يَسْقِيَهُ مِنْ رَدْغَةِ الْخَبَالِ يَوْمَ الْقِيَامَةِ ‏"‏ ‏.‏ قَالُوا يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ وَمَا رَدْغَةُ الْخَبَالِ قَالَ ‏"‏ عُصَارَةُ أَهْلِ النَّارِ

"Whoever drinks wine and gets drunk, no prayer is accepted from him forty mornings; and if he dies, he enters the Fire; and if he repents, God turns to him. And if he returns, and drinks and gets drunk, no prayer is accepted from him forty mornings; and if he dies, he enters the Fire; and if he repents, God turns to him. And if he returns, and drinks and gets drunk, no prayer is accepted from him forty mornings; and if he dies, he enters the Fire; and if he repents, God turns to him. And if he returns, it is a right upon God that He give him to drink of the radghat al-khabal on the Day of Resurrection." They said: "O Messenger of God, and what is the radghat al-khabal?" He said: "The pressings of the people of the Fire."

**Other bars.**
- **Lying about the Prophet** is in Bukhari 106 to 110.
- **The lote-tree.** Not in Bukhari or Muslim: "Whoever cuts a lote-tree (sidra), God plunges his head into the Fire" (Abu Dawud 5239; al-Albani Sahih, Zai Hasan).
- **Black dye.** Also not in either: those who dye black "will not smell the scent of the Garden" (Abu Dawud 4212; al-Albani Sahih, Zai isnad Sahih).

#### The seventy-three sects

Seven reports carry the split. None is in Bukhari or Muslim.
- **Abu Hurayra's versions** (Abu Dawud 4596, Tirmidhi 2640, Ibn Majah 3991; al-Albani Hasan Sahih, Zai isnad Hasan) give the count only. They name no Fire and no saved sect.
- **The community (al-jama'a).** Three versions name the saved one so:

**Abu Dawud 4597** (from Mu'awiya b. Abi Sufyan; also ibnmajah:3992; ibnmajah:3993; tirmidhi:2641; grade: Al-Albani: Hasan; Muhammad Muhyi Al-Din Abdul Hamid: Hasan; Zubair Ali Zai: Isnaad Hasan)

> أَلاَ إِنَّ مَنْ قَبْلَكُمْ مِنْ أَهْلِ الْكِتَابِ افْتَرَقُوا عَلَى ثِنْتَيْنِ وَسَبْعِينَ مِلَّةً وَإِنَّ هَذِهِ الْمِلَّةَ سَتَفْتَرِقُ عَلَى ثَلاَثٍ وَسَبْعِينَ ثِنْتَانِ وَسَبْعُونَ فِي النَّارِ وَوَاحِدَةٌ فِي الْجَنَّةِ وَهِيَ الْجَمَاعَةُ ‏"‏ ‏.‏ زَادَ ابْنُ يَحْيَى وَعَمْرٌو فِي حَدِيثَيْهِمَا ‏"‏ وَإِنَّهُ سَيَخْرُجُ مِنْ أُمَّتِي أَقْوَامٌ تَجَارَى بِهِمْ تِلْكَ الأَهْوَاءُ كَمَا يَتَجَارَى الْكَلْبُ لِصَاحِبِهِ ‏"‏ ‏.‏ وَقَالَ عَمْرٌو ‏"‏ الْكَلْبُ بِصَاحِبِهِ لاَ يَبْقَى مِنْهُ عِرْقٌ وَلاَ مَفْصِلٌ إِلاَّ دَخَلَهُ

"Indeed those before you of the People of the Book split into seventy-two creeds (milla), and this creed will split into seventy-three: seventy-two in the Fire and one in the Garden, and it is the community (al-jama'a)." Ibn Yahya and 'Amr added in their reports: "And there will come out of my community peoples in whom those desires (al-ahwa') run as rabies (al-kalab) runs in its victim." And 'Amr said: "... in its victim: there remains of him no vein and no joint but it has entered it."

**Ibn Majah 3992** (from 'Awf b. Malik; grade: Al-Albani: Sahih; Muhammad Fouad Abd al-Baqi: Sahih; Shuaib Al Arnaut: Sahih Lighairihi; Zubair Ali Zai: Isnaad Hasan)

> افْتَرَقَتِ الْيَهُودُ عَلَى إِحْدَى وَسَبْعِينَ فِرْقَةً فَوَاحِدَةٌ فِي الْجَنَّةِ وَسَبْعُونَ فِي النَّارِ وَافْتَرَقَتِ النَّصَارَى عَلَى ثِنْتَيْنِ وَسَبْعِينَ فِرْقَةً فَإِحْدَى وَسَبْعُونَ فِي النَّارِ وَوَاحِدَةٌ فِي الْجَنَّةِ وَالَّذِي نَفْسُ مُحَمَّدٍ بِيَدِهِ لَتَفْتَرِقَنَّ أُمَّتِي عَلَى ثَلاَثٍ وَسَبْعِينَ فِرْقَةً فَوَاحِدَةٌ فِي الْجَنَّةِ وَثِنْتَانِ وَسَبْعُونَ فِي النَّارِ ‏"‏ ‏.‏ قِيلَ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ مَنْ هُمْ قَالَ ‏"‏ الْجَمَاعَةُ

The Jews split into seventy-one sects: one in the Garden and seventy in the Fire. And the Christians split into seventy-two sects: seventy-one in the Fire and one in the Garden. And by the One in whose hand is the soul of Muhammad, my community will split into seventy-three sects: one in the Garden and seventy-two in the Fire. It was said: "O Messenger of God, who are they?" He said: "The community (al-jama'a)."

- **Seventy-two.** Ibn Majah 3993 (Anas) gives seventy-two, "all of them in the Fire except one, and it is the community (al-jama'a)" (al-Albani, 'Abd al-Baqi and Zai Sahih).
- **The Prophet and his Companions.** One version names the saved one by them:

**Tirmidhi 2641** (from 'Abd Allah b. 'Amr b. al-'As; grade: Ahmad Muhammad Shakir: Hasan; Al-Albani: Hasan; Zubair Ali Zai: Daif; Tirmidhi: هَذَا حَدِيثٌ مُفَسَّرٌ حَسَنٌ غَرِيبٌ لاَ نَعْرِفُهُ مِثْلَ هَذَا إِلاَّ مِنْ هَذَا الْوَجْهِ "this is a hadith explained (mufassar), hasan gharib; we do not know [it] like this except from this route")

> لَيَأْتِيَنَّ عَلَى أُمَّتِي مَا أَتَى عَلَى بَنِي إِسْرَائِيلَ حَذْوَ النَّعْلِ بِالنَّعْلِ حَتَّى إِنْ كَانَ مِنْهُمْ مَنْ أَتَى أُمَّهُ عَلاَنِيَةً لَكَانَ فِي أُمَّتِي مَنْ يَصْنَعُ ذَلِكَ وَإِنَّ بَنِي إِسْرَائِيلَ تَفَرَّقَتْ عَلَى ثِنْتَيْنِ وَسَبْعِينَ مِلَّةً وَتَفْتَرِقُ أُمَّتِي عَلَى ثَلاَثٍ وَسَبْعِينَ مِلَّةً كُلُّهُمْ فِي النَّارِ إِلاَّ مِلَّةً وَاحِدَةً قَالُوا وَمَنْ هِيَ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ قَالَ مَا أَنَا عَلَيْهِ وَأَصْحَابِي

There will come upon my community what came upon the Children of Israel, sandal for sandal, so that if there was among them one who came to his mother openly, there would be in my community one who does that. And the Children of Israel split into seventy-two creeds (milla), and my community will split into seventy-three creeds, all of them in the Fire except one creed. They said: "And which is it, O Messenger of God?" He said: "What I am upon, and my Companions."

#### Grace, mercy and intercession

**Abu Dawud 4739** (from Anas b. Malik; also tirmidhi:2435; ibnmajah:4310; grade: Al-Albani: Sahih; Muhammad Muhyi Al-Din Abdul Hamid: Sahih; Shuaib Al Arnaut: Sahih; Zubair Ali Zai: Sahih)

> شَفَاعَتِي لأَهْلِ الْكَبَائِرِ مِنْ أُمَّتِي

"My intercession is for the people of the great [sins] of my community."

Also Tirmidhi 2435 (Sahih) and Ibn Majah 4310 (al-Albani Sahih, Zai Hasan). Bukhari and Muslim do not carry it.

**Tirmidhi 2441** (from 'Awf b. Malik al-Ashja'i; also ibnmajah:4311; ibnmajah:4317; grade: Ahmad Muhammad Shakir: Sahih; Al-Albani: Sahih; Zubair Ali Zai: Hasan)

> أَتَانِي آتٍ مِنْ عِنْدِ رَبِّي فَخَيَّرَنِي بَيْنَ أَنْ يُدْخِلَ نِصْفَ أُمَّتِي الْجَنَّةَ وَبَيْنَ الشَّفَاعَةِ فَاخْتَرْتُ الشَّفَاعَةَ وَهِيَ لِمَنْ مَاتَ لاَ يُشْرِكُ بِاللَّهِ شَيْئًا

"One came to me from my Lord and gave me the choice between [His] admitting half of my community to the Garden and the intercession; so I chose the intercession, and it is for whoever died not associating anything with God."

Also Ibn Majah 4317 (Sahih). Others intercede:
- **The martyr.** "The martyr intercedes for seventy of the people of his house" (Abu Dawud 2522; al-Albani Sahih, Zai Hasan).
- **One man.** By one man's intercession "more than the Banu Tamim" enter (Ibn Majah 4316; Sahih).
- **A fabricated ranking.** "Three intercede on the Day of Resurrection: the prophets, then the learned, then the martyrs" (Ibn Majah 4313; al-Albani and 'Abd al-Baqi Mawdu').

**Tirmidhi 3225** (from Abu Sa'id al-Khudri; grade: Ahmad Muhammad Shakir: Sahih; Al-Albani: Sahih; Zubair Ali Zai: Daif; Tirmidhi: هَذَا حَدِيثٌ حَسَنٌ غَرِيبٌ لاَ نَعْرِفُهُ إِلاَّ مِنْ هَذَا الْوَجْهِ "this is a hadith hasan gharib; we do not know it except from this route")

> ثمَّ أَوْرَثْنَا الْكِتَابَ الَّذِينَ اصْطَفَيْنَا مِنْ عِبَادِنَا فَمِنْهُمْ ظَالِمٌ لِنَفْسِهِ وَمِنْهُمْ مُقْتَصِدٌ وَمِنْهُمْ سَابِقٌ بِالْخَيْرَاتِ ‏)‏ قَالَ ‏"‏ هَؤُلاَءِ كُلُّهُمْ بِمَنْزِلَةٍ وَاحِدَةٍ وَكُلُّهُمْ فِي الْجَنَّةِ

On this verse: "Then We gave the Book as inheritance to those We chose of Our servants; so of them is one who wrongs himself, and of them one moderate, and of them one foremost in good deeds" (35:32), he said: "These, all of them, are at one station, and all of them are in the Garden."

**Ibn Majah 4292** (from Anas b. Malik; also muslim:2767 (the ransom only, from Abu Musa); grade: Al-Albani: Sahih; Muhammad Fouad Abd al-Baqi: Sahih; Zubair Ali Zai: Daif)

> إِنَّ هَذِهِ أُمَّةٌ مَرْحُومَةٌ عَذَابُهَا بِأَيْدِيهَا فَإِذَا كَانَ يَوْمُ الْقِيَامَةِ دُفِعَ إِلَى كُلِّ رَجُلٍ مِنَ الْمُسْلِمِينَ رَجُلٌ مِنَ الْمُشْرِكِينَ فَيُقَالُ هَذَا فِدَاؤُكَ مِنَ النَّارِ

This is a community shown mercy (marhuma); its punishment is by its own hands. And when the Day of Resurrection comes, there is handed to every man of the Muslims a man of the associators, and it is said: "This is your ransom (fida') from the Fire."

The ransom alone is in Muslim 2767. "None enters the Fire except a wretched [one] (shaqi)" (Ibn Majah 4298; weak, Da'if by all four graders).

#### What the Garden is said to be

**Degrees.**

**Tirmidhi 2531** (from 'Ubada b. al-Samit; also tirmidhi:2530; bukhari:2790; grade: Ahmad Muhammad Shakir: Sahih; Al-Albani: Sahih; Zubair Ali Zai: Isnaad Sahih)

> فِي الْجَنَّةِ مِائَةُ دَرَجَةٍ مَا بَيْنَ كُلِّ دَرَجَتَيْنِ كَمَا بَيْنَ السَّمَاءِ وَالأَرْضِ وَالْفِرْدَوْسُ أَعْلاَهَا دَرَجَةً وَمِنْهَا تُفَجَّرُ أَنْهَارُ الْجَنَّةِ الأَرْبَعَةُ وَمِنْ فَوْقِهَا يَكُونُ الْعَرْشُ فَإِذَا سَأَلْتُمُ اللَّهَ فَسَلُوهُ الْفِرْدَوْسَ

In the Garden are a hundred degrees; between every two degrees [is] as between the heaven and the earth; and al-Firdaws is the highest of them in degree, and from it the four rivers of the Garden burst forth, and above it is the Throne. So when you ask God, ask Him for al-Firdaws.

Also Bukhari 2790, the hundred degrees and al-Firdaws.

**The market.**

**Tirmidhi 2550** (from 'Ali b. Abi Talib; grade: Ahmad Muhammad Shakir: Daif; Al-Albani: Daif; Zubair Ali Zai: Daif; Tirmidhi: هَذَا حَدِيثٌ غَرِيبٌ "this is a hadith gharib")

> إِنَّ فِي الْجَنَّةِ لَسُوقًا مَا فِيهَا شِرَاءٌ وَلاَ بَيْعٌ إِلاَّ الصُّوَرَ مِنَ الرِّجَالِ وَالنِّسَاءِ فَإِذَا اشْتَهَى الرَّجُلُ صُورَةً دَخَلَ فِيهَا

Indeed in the Garden is a market; there is no buying in it nor selling, except the forms (al-suwar) of men and women; when a man desires a form, he enters into it.

Tirmidhi 2549 and Ibn Majah 4336 add a Friday visit to the Lord (weak, Da'if by every grader). Muslim 2833 has a Friday market only.

**Seas and rivers.**

**Tirmidhi 2571** (from Mu'awiya b. Hayda; grade: Ahmad Muhammad Shakir: Sahih; Al-Albani: Sahih; Bashar Awad Maarouf: Hasan Sahih; Zubair Ali Zai: Hasan; Tirmidhi: هَذَا حَدِيثٌ حَسَنٌ صَحِيحٌ "this is a hadith hasan sahih")

> إِنَّ فِي الْجَنَّةِ بَحْرَ الْمَاءِ وَبَحْرَ الْعَسَلِ وَبَحْرَ اللَّبَنِ وَبَحْرَ الْخَمْرِ ثُمَّ تُشَقَّقُ الأَنْهَارُ بَعْدُ

Indeed in the Garden is the sea of water and the sea of honey and the sea of milk and the sea of wine; then the rivers split off after.

Of al-Kawthar: "its two banks are of gold, and its course is over pearls and rubies" (Tirmidhi 3361; al-Albani Sahih, Zai Hasan; in part Bukhari 4964).

**Wives, the hur and bodies.**

**Tirmidhi 2562** (from Abu Sa'id al-Khudri; grade: Ahmad Muhammad Shakir: Daif; Al-Albani: Daif; Zubair Ali Zai: Hasan; Tirmidhi: هَذَا حَدِيثٌ غَرِيبٌ لاَ نَعْرِفُهُ إِلاَّ مِنْ حَدِيثِ رِشْدِينَ بْنِ سَعْدٍ "this is a hadith gharib; we do not know it except from the hadith of Rishdin b. Sa'd")

> أَدْنَى أَهْلِ الْجَنَّةِ الَّذِي لَهُ ثَمَانُونَ أَلْفَ خَادِمٍ وَاثْنَتَانِ وَسَبْعُونَ زَوْجَةً وَتُنْصَبُ لَهُ قُبَّةٌ مِنْ لُؤْلُؤٍ وَزَبَرْجَدٍ وَيَاقُوتٍ كَمَا بَيْنَ الْجَابِيَةِ إِلَى صَنْعَاءَ ‏"‏ ‏.‏ وَبِهَذَا الإِسْنَادِ عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏"‏ مَنْ مَاتَ مِنْ أَهْلِ الْجَنَّةِ مِنْ صَغِيرٍ أَوْ كَبِيرٍ يُرَدُّونَ بَنِي ثَلاَثِينَ فِي الْجَنَّةِ لاَ يَزِيدُونَ عَلَيْهَا أَبَدًا وَكَذَلِكَ أَهْلُ النَّارِ ‏"‏ ‏.‏ وَبِهَذَا الإِسْنَادِ عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏"‏ إِنَّ عَلَيْهِمُ التِّيجَانَ إِنَّ أَدْنَى لُؤْلُؤَةٍ مِنْهَا لَتُضِيءُ مَا بَيْنَ الْمَشْرِقِ وَالْمَغْرِبِ

The lowest of the people of the Garden is he who has eighty thousand servants and seventy-two wives, and there is set up for him a dome of pearl and chrysolite and ruby, as [wide as] between al-Jabiya and San'a'. And with this isnad from the Prophet, he said: "Whoever dies of the people of the Garden, small or great, they are returned [as] sons of thirty in the Garden; they never increase beyond it; and likewise the people of the Fire." And with this isnad from the Prophet, he said: "On them are the crowns; the least pearl of them would light up what is between the east and the west."

Ibn Majah 4337 (Abu Umama) is very weak: al-Albani, 'Abd al-Baqi and al-Arna'ut call it Very Da'if. It reads: ar: قَالَ هِشَامُ بْنُ خَالِدٍ مِنْ مِيرَاثِهِ مِنْ أَهْلِ النَّارِ يَعْنِي رِجَالاً دَخَلُوا النَّارَ فَوَرِثَ أَهْلُ الْجَنَّةِ نِسَاءَهُمْ كَمَا وُرِثَتِ امْرَأَةُ فِرْعَوْنَ There is no one whom God admits to the Garden but God, mighty and majestic, marries him to seventy-two wives: two of the white-eyed, wide-eyed [ones] (hur 'in), and seventy of his inheritance from the people of the Fire. There is not one of them but she has a desirous vulva (qubul shahi), and he has a member that does not bend. [The transmitter] Hisham b. Khalid said: "Of his inheritance from the people of the Fire" means men who entered the Fire, and the people of the Garden inherited their women, as the wife of Pharaoh was inherited.
- **Strength.** The believer "is given the strength of a hundred" (Tirmidhi 2536; al-Albani Hasan Sahih, Zai Da'if).
- **Age.** The people of the Garden enter "hairless, beardless, kohl-eyed, sons of thirty or of thirty-three years" (Tirmidhi 2545; al-Albani Hasan, Zai Da'if).

**The lowest, the highest and the vision.**

**Tirmidhi 2553** (from 'Abd Allah b. 'Umar; also tirmidhi:3330; grade: Ahmad Muhammad Shakir: Daif; Al-Albani: Daif; Zubair Ali Zai: Daif)

> إِنَّ أَدْنَى أَهْلِ الْجَنَّةِ مَنْزِلَةً لَمَنْ يَنْظُرُ إِلَى جِنَانِهِ وَأَزْوَاجِهِ وَنَعِيمِهِ وَخَدَمِهِ وَسُرُرِهِ مَسِيرَةَ أَلْفِ سَنَةٍ وَأَكْرَمَهُمْ عَلَى اللَّهِ مَنْ يَنْظُرُ إِلَى وَجْهِهِ غُدْوَةً وَعَشِيَّةً ‏"‏ ‏.‏ ثُمَّ قَرَأَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم‏:‏ ‏(‏وُجُوهٌ يَوْمَئِذٍ نَاضِرَةٌ * إِلَى رَبِّهَا نَاظِرَةٌ

Indeed the lowest of the people of the Garden in station is one who looks at his gardens and his wives and his bliss and his servants and his couches [over] a journey of a thousand years; and the most honoured of them with God is one who looks at His face morning and evening. Then the Messenger of God recited: "Faces that day radiant, looking at their Lord" (75:22 to 23).

Most vision reports have the Bukhari and Muslim wording: Tirmidhi 2552 and Ibn Majah 187 are Muslim 181. Ibn Majah 184 is not in either: "the Lord has looked down upon them from above them" (weak, Da'if by all four graders).

#### The language of exchange

**Tirmidhi 2450** (from Abu Hurayra; grade: Ahmad Muhammad Shakir: Sahih; Al-Albani: Sahih; Zubair Ali Zai: Daif; Tirmidhi: هَذَا حَدِيثٌ حَسَنٌ غَرِيبٌ لاَ نَعْرِفُهُ إِلاَّ مِنْ حَدِيثِ أَبِي النَّضْرِ "this is a hadith hasan gharib; we know it only from the hadith of Abu l-Nadr")

> مَنْ خَافَ أَدْلَجَ وَمَنْ أَدْلَجَ بَلَغَ الْمَنْزِلَ أَلاَ إِنَّ سِلْعَةَ اللَّهِ غَالِيَةٌ أَلاَ إِنَّ سِلْعَةَ اللَّهِ الْجَنَّةُ

"Whoever fears sets out at night (adlaja), and whoever sets out at night reaches the stage (al-manzil). Lo! the merchandise (sil'a) of God is costly (ghaliya). Lo! the merchandise of God is the Garden."

**Ibn Majah 1837** (from Thawban; also abudawud:1643; nasai:2590; grade: Al-Albani: Sahih; Muhammad Fouad Abd al-Baqi: Sahih; Zubair Ali Zai: Isnaad Sahih)

> مَنْ يَتَقَبَّلُ لِي بِوَاحِدَةٍ وَأَتَقَبَّلُ لَهُ بِالْجَنَّةِ ‏"‏ قُلْتُ أَنَا ‏.‏ قَالَ ‏"‏ لاَ تَسْأَلِ النَّاسَ شَيْئًا ‏"‏ ‏.‏ قَالَ فَكَانَ ثَوْبَانُ يَقَعُ سَوْطُهُ وَهُوَ رَاكِبٌ فَلاَ يَقُولُ لأَحَدٍ نَاوِلْنِيهِ حَتَّى يَنْزِلَ فَيَأْخُذَهُ

"Who will take upon himself for me one [thing], and I take upon myself for him the Garden?" I said: "I." He said: "Do not ask people for anything." He said: So Thawban's whip would fall while he was riding, and he would not say to anyone "Hand it to me," until he dismounted and took it.

**Ibn Majah 4341** (from Abu Hurayra; grade: Al-Albani: Sahih; Muhammad Fouad Abd al-Baqi: Sahih; Shuaib Al Arnaut: Sahih; Zubair Ali Zai: Daif)

> مَا مِنْكُمْ مِنْ أَحَدٍ إِلاَّ لَهُ مَنْزِلاَنِ مَنْزِلٌ فِي الْجَنَّةِ وَمَنْزِلٌ فِي النَّارِ فَإِذَا مَاتَ فَدَخَلَ النَّارَ وَرِثَ أَهْلُ الْجَنَّةِ مَنْزِلَهُ فَذَلِكَ قَوْلُهُ تَعَالَى ‏{أُولَئِكَ هُمُ الْوَارِثُونَ

There is none of you but he has two dwellings: a dwelling in the Garden and a dwelling in the Fire. So when he dies and enters the Fire, the people of the Garden inherit his dwelling. That is His saying, exalted: "Those, they are the inheritors" (23:10).

- **The well.** "Who will buy the well of Ruma and put his bucket with the buckets of the Muslims, for better than it in the Garden?" (Tirmidhi 3703; Hasan by all four graders; in part Bukhari 2778).
- **The ledger.** On the gate of the Garden: "Charity [is repaid] tenfold, and the loan eighteenfold" (Ibn Majah 2431; very weak, al-Albani Very Da'if).

### 2.12 Twelver Shia hadith

#### Sources, grading and the sweep

The Arabic is from thaqalayn.net (open API, fetched 2026-11-19) for four works, with the OpenITI texts of *al-Kafi*, *Thawab* and *Kamil* as a second witness:
- al-Kulayni's *al-Kafi*;
- Ibn Babawayh's *Thawab al-a'mal* and *Man la yahduruhu al-faqih*;
- Ibn Qulawayh's *Kamil al-ziyarat*.

*al-Kafi* is cited as volume:page:number of the Tehran edition, from the OpenITI page markers. *Thawab* and the *Faqih* are cited by the thaqalayn number, and *Kamil* by chapter and number.

Each *al-Kafi* report carries al-Majlisi's grade from *Mir'at al-'uqul*, as thaqalayn shows it, and "not sound" marks his weak, unknown, mursal and marfu' grades. thaqalayn gives no grade for the other three works.

The sweep read 1,231 reports and kept 754 after repeats were merged:
- 863 were search hits;
- 368 more were read with their chapters;
- 292 of the 468 kept *al-Kafi* reports are not sound.

Every Arabic excerpt below was checked by machine as an exact substring of its source file. The working file is `ext-ref/religion_ends/hadith_shia.md`.

#### The conditions, in order of weight

| Condition | Kept reports |
|---|---|
| Wilaya: knowing, obeying and taking the Imam as guardian | 151 |
| Remembrance, du'a and the Qur'an | 103 |
| Deeds toward a believing brother | 87 |
| Visiting al-Husayn's grave (below) | 74 |
| Intercession (below) | 68 |
| Fear of God, patience, affliction | 63 |
| Pilgrimage | 61 |
| Prayer | 49 |
| Belief and the shahada | 43 |
| Jihad (below) | 41 |
| Visiting the Prophet and the other Imams | 40 |
| Alms and spending | 38 |
| Fasting | 37 |
| Love of the Ahl al-Bayt | 36 |
| Good character | 29 |
| Weeping for al-Husayn (below) | 21 |
| Kinship | 20 |

**Wilaya.** Five reports of the chapter *Da'a'im al-islam* build Islam on five, with the wilaya in place of the shahada.

**kafi:2:18:1** (from Abu Ja'far [Muhammad al-Baqir]; grade: al-Majlisi: ضعيف على المشهور (weak according to the well-known [view]); Mirʾāt al-ʿUqūl fī Sharḥ Akhbār Āl al-Rasūl (7/100))

> بُنِيَ الإسْلامُ عَلَى خَمْسٍ عَلَى الصَّلاةِ وَالزَّكَاةِ وَالصَّوْمِ وَالْحَجِّ وَالْوَلايَةِ وَلَمْ يُنَادَ بِشَيْ‏ءٍ كَمَا نُودِيَ بِالْوَلايَةِ

Islam is built on five: on the prayer, and the alms (al-zakat), and the fast, and the pilgrimage, and the wilaya; and nothing was called out as the wilaya was called out.

Zurara's report opens with the same five, and calls the wilaya the best, as their key. Its close states what deeds are worth without it.

**kafi:2:18:5** (from Abu Ja'far [Muhammad al-Baqir]; grade: al-Majlisi: صحيح (sound); Mirʾāt al-ʿUqūl fī Sharḥ Akhbār Āl al-Rasūl (7/102))

> ذِرْوَةُ الأمْرِ وَسَنَامُهُ وَمِفْتَاحُهُ وَبَابُ الأشْيَاءِ وَرِضَا الرَّحْمَنِ الطَّاعَةُ لِلإمَامِ بَعْدَ مَعْرِفَتِهِ إِنَّ الله عَزَّ وَجَلَّ يَقُولُ مَنْ يُطِعِ الرَّسُولَ فَقَدْ أَطاعَ الله وَمَنْ تَوَلَّى فَما أَرْسَلْناكَ عَلَيْهِمْ حَفِيظاً أَمَا لَوْ أَنَّ رَجُلاً قَامَ لَيْلَهُ وَصَامَ نَهَارَهُ وَتَصَدَّقَ بِجَمِيعِ مَالِهِ وَحَجَّ جَمِيعَ دَهْرِهِ وَلَمْ يَعْرِفْ وَلايَةَ وَلِيِّ الله فَيُوَالِيَهُ وَيَكُونَ جَمِيعُ أَعْمَالِهِ بِدَلالَتِهِ إِلَيْهِ مَا كَانَ لَهُ عَلَى الله جَلَّ وَعَزَّ حَقٌّ فِي ثَوَابِهِ وَلا كَانَ مِنْ أَهْلِ الإيمَانِ ثُمَّ قَالَ أُولَئِكَ الْمُحْسِنُ مِنْهُمْ يُدْخِلُهُ الله الْجَنَّةَ بِفَضْلِ رَحْمَتِهِ

The summit of the matter and its hump and its key and the gate of things and the good pleasure of the Merciful [is] obedience to the Imam after knowing him. Indeed God, Mighty and Majestic, says: 'Whoever obeys the Messenger has obeyed God; and whoever turns away, We did not send you over them as a keeper' [4:80]. Indeed, if a man stood [in prayer] his night, and fasted his day, and gave in alms all his wealth, and made pilgrimage all his time, and did not know the wilaya of the guardian (wali) of God, so as to take him as guardian, and [so that] all his deeds be by his guidance to Him, he would not have had over God, Majestic and Mighty, a right to His reward, nor would he have been of the people of iman. Then he said: Those, the doer of good among them, God makes him enter the Garden by the bounty of His mercy.

**kafi:1:183:8** (from Abu Ja'far [Muhammad al-Baqir]; grade: al-Majlisi: صحيح (sound); Mirʾāt al-ʿUqūl fī Sharḥ Akhbār Āl al-Rasūl (2/313))

> كُلُّ مَنْ دَانَ الله عَزَّ وَجَلَّ بِعِبَادَةٍ يُجْهِدُ فِيهَا نَفْسَهُ وَلا إِمَامَ لَهُ مِنَ الله فَسَعْيُهُ غَيْرُ مَقْبُولٍ وَهُوَ ضَالٌّ مُتَحَيِّرٌ وَالله شَانِئٌ لاعْمَالِهِ

Everyone who served God with a worship in which he exerts himself, and there is no Imam for him from God, then his effort is not accepted, and he is astray, bewildered, and God hates his deeds.

**thawab:806** (from Abu 'Abd Allah [al-Sadiq]; grade: no grade given (thaqalayn.net gives no grading for Thawab al-a'mal))

> یَا مَیْسَرَهُ مَا بَیْنَ الرُّکْنِ وَ الْمَقَامِ رَوْضَهٌ مِنْ رِیَاضِ الْجَنَّهِ وَ اللَّهِ لَوْ أَنَّ عَبْداً عَمَّرَهُ اللَّهُ فِیمَا بَیْنَ الرُّکْنِ وَ الْمَقَامِ أَلْفَ عَامٍ وَ فِیمَا بَیْنَ الْقَبْرِ وَ الْمِنْبَرِ یَعْبُدُهُ أَلْفَ عَامٍ ثُمَّ ذُبِحَ عَلَی فِرَاشِهِ مَظْلُوماً کَمَا یُذْبَحُ الْکَبْشُ الْأَمْلَحُ ثُمَّ لَقِیَ اللَّهَ عَزَّ وَ جَلَّ بِغَیْرِ وَلَایَتِنَا لَکَانَ حَقِیقاً عَلَی اللَّهِ عَزَّ وَ جَلَّ أَنْ یُکِبَّهُ عَلَی مَنْخِرَیْهِ فِی نَارِ جَهَنَّمَ

O Maysara, what is between the Corner and the Station [is] a meadow of the meadows of the Garden. By God, if a servant were given life by God, between the Corner and the Station, a thousand years, and between the grave and the pulpit, worshipping Him a thousand years, then were slaughtered on his bed, wronged, as the white ram is slaughtered, then met God without our wilaya, it would be due upon God that He throw him on his nostrils into the Fire of Hell.

- Those who fast and pray but resolve not to refer matters to the Imams "would be associators (mushrikin) by that" (kafi:2:398:5, unknown, not sound).
- Islam spares blood, "and the reward is on iman" (kafi:2:24:1, unknown or rather good).

God judges a people by the imam it serves.

**kafi:1:376:4** (from Abu Ja'far [Muhammad al-Baqir], quoting God; grade: al-Majlisi: صحيح (sound); Mirʾāt al-ʿUqūl fī Sharḥ Akhbār Āl al-Rasūl (4/218))

> لاعَذِّبَنَّ كُلَّ رَعِيَّةٍ فِي الاسْلامِ دَانَتْ بِوَلايَةِ كُلِّ إِمَامٍ جَائِرٍ لَيْسَ مِنَ الله وَإِنْ كَانَتِ الرَّعِيَّةُ فِي أَعْمَالِهَا بَرَّةً تَقِيَّةً وَلاعْفُوَنَّ عَنْ كُلِّ رَعِيَّةٍ فِي الاسْلامِ دَانَتْ بِوَلايَةِ كُلِّ إِمَامٍ عَادِلٍ مِنَ الله وَإِنْ كَانَتِ الرَّعِيَّةُ فِي أَنْفُسِهَا ظَالِمَةً مُسِيئَةً

I will surely punish every subject people (ra'iyya) in Islam that served [Me] by the wilaya of every unjust imam who is not from God, even if the subject people were, in their deeds, righteous, God-fearing; and I will surely pardon every subject people in Islam that served [Me] by the wilaya of every just Imam from God, even if the subject people were, in themselves, wrongdoing, evil-doing.

**Dying without the Imam.** Nine *al-Kafi* reports call it a death of the Jahiliyya. One glosses it "A death of error (dalal)" (kafi:1:376:2, weak by the well-known view). Another glosses it more strongly:

**kafi:1:377:3** (from Abu 'Abd Allah [Ja'far al-Sadiq], on a saying of the Messenger of God; grade: al-Majlisi: صحيح (sound); Mirʾāt al-ʿUqūl fī Sharḥ Akhbār Āl al-Rasūl (4/220))

> قَالَ رَسُولُ الله (صَلَّى اللهُ عَلَيْهِ وَآلِه) مَنْ مَاتَ لا يَعْرِفُ إِمَامَهُ مَاتَ مِيتَةً جَاهِلِيَّةً قَالَ نَعَمْ قُلْتُ جَاهِلِيَّةً جَهْلاءَ أَوْ جَاهِلِيَّةً لا يَعْرِفُ إِمَامَهُ قَالَ جَاهِلِيَّةَ كُفْرٍ وَنِفَاقٍ وَضَلالٍ

[I said:] The Messenger of God said, "Whoever died not knowing his Imam died a death of the Jahiliyya." He said: "Yes." I said: "A Jahiliyya utterly ignorant, or a Jahiliyya [of one who] does not know his Imam?" He said: "A Jahiliyya of unbelief and hypocrisy and error."

al-Sadiq calls the form current among the common people "The truth, by God" (kafi:1:378:2, apparently good).

**The shahada narrowed.** The shahada's promise stands, and is then restricted.

**kafi:2:520:1** (from Abu 'Abd Allah [Ja'far al-Sadiq]; grade: al-Majlisi: موثق (reliable); Mirʾāt al-ʿUqūl fī Sharḥ Akhbār Āl al-Rasūl (12/209))

> يَا أَبَانُ إِذَا قَدِمْتَ الْكُوفَةَ فَارْوِ هَذَا الْحَدِيثَ مَنْ شَهِدَ أَنْ لا إِلَهَ إِلا الله مُخْلِصاً وَجَبَتْ لَهُ الْجَنَّةُ قَالَ قُلْتُ لَهُ إِنَّهُ يَأْتِينِي مِنْ كُلِّ صِنْفٍ مِنَ الأصْنَافِ أَ فَأَرْوِي لَهُمْ هَذَا الْحَدِيثَ قَالَ نَعَمْ يَا أَبَانُ إِنَّهُ إِذَا كَانَ يَوْمُ الْقِيَامَةِ وَجَمَعَ الله الأوَّلِينَ وَالآخِرِينَ فَتُسْلَبُ لا إِلَهَ إِلا الله مِنْهُمْ إِلا مَنْ كَانَ عَلَى هَذَا الأمْرِ

O Aban, when you come to Kufa, relate this report: Whoever testifies that there is no god but God, sincerely, the Garden is due to him. He said: I said to him: People of every kind of the kinds come to me; shall I relate this report to them? He said: Yes, O Aban. When the Day of Resurrection is, and God has gathered the first and the last, 'There is no god but God' is stripped from them, except whoever was upon this affair.

The testimony "is accepted only from this [man] and from his Shi'a" (thawab:26, no grade given). The dead are asked in the grave "Who is your Imam?" (kafi:3:238:11, mursal, not sound).

**Deeds toward a believing brother.** These are the largest group of conduct reports: visiting, feeding, clothing, and meeting a believer's need. Some limit the reward by the Imam.

**kafi:2:192:1** (from Abu 'Abd Allah [Ja'far al-Sadiq]; grade: al-Majlisi: ضعيف على المشهور (weak according to the well-known [view]); Mirʾāt al-ʿUqūl fī Sharḥ Akhbār Āl al-Rasūl (9/101))

> وَمَنْ قَضَى لأخِيهِ الْمُؤْمِنِ حَاجَةً قَضَى الله عَزَّ وَجَلَّ لَهُ يَوْمَ الْقِيَامَةِ مِائَةَ أَلْفِ حَاجَةٍ مِنْ ذَلِكَ أَوَّلُهَا الْجَنَّةُ وَمِنْ ذَلِكَ أَنْ يُدْخِلَ قَرَابَتَهُ وَمَعَارِفَهُ وَإِخْوَانَهُ الْجَنَّةَ بَعْدَ أَنْ لا يَكُونُوا نُصَّاباً

And whoever fulfils for his believing brother a need, God, Mighty and Majestic, fulfils for him on the Day of Resurrection a hundred thousand needs; of that, the first of them [is] the Garden; and of that, that He makes his kin and his acquaintances and his brothers enter the Garden, provided that they are not nasibis (nussab).

**Love of the Ahl al-Bayt.** "Love of us [is] belief, and hatred of us [is] unbelief" (kafi:1:187:12, unknown, not sound). Love of the Shi'a admits to the Garden without knowledge of their creed.

**kafi:2:126:10** (from Abu 'Abd Allah [Ja'far al-Sadiq]; grade: al-Majlisi: حسن كالصحيح (good, like sound); Mirʾāt al-ʿUqūl fī Sharḥ Akhbār Āl al-Rasūl (8/263))

> إِنَّ الرَّجُلَ لَيُحِبُّكُمْ وَمَا يَعْرِفُ مَا أَنْتُمْ عَلَيْهِ فَيُدْخِلُهُ الله الْجَنَّةَ بِحُبِّكُمْ وَإِنَّ الرَّجُلَ لَيُبْغِضُكُمْ وَمَا يَعْرِفُ مَا أَنْتُمْ عَلَيْهِ فَيُدْخِلُهُ الله بِبُغْضِكُمُ النَّارَ

Indeed a man loves you and does not know what you are on, and God makes him enter the Garden by his love of you; and indeed a man hates you and does not know what you are on, and God makes him enter, by his hatred of you, the Fire.

Another report answers that "there is not with us an immunity (bara'a) from the Fire", and that "our wilaya is not attained except by deed and scruple" (kafi:2:74:3, weak).

**The pillars and character.** These have the form of the Sunni books of virtues:
- **Prayer.** Prayer kept at its times gives "a covenant ('ahd) by which He admits him into the Garden" (kafi:3:267:1, sound).
- **Pilgrimage.** "The pilgrimage, its reward is the Garden" (kafi:4:253:4, weak by the well-known view).
- **Character.** "The most of what my community enters the Garden by [is] fear of God (taqwa) and good character" (kafi:2:100:6, weak by the well-known view).

#### What bars the Garden

**Hostility to the Imams (84 reports).** No intercession reaches the nasib.

**thawab:794** (from Abu 'Abd Allah [al-Sadiq]; grade: no grade given (thaqalayn.net gives no grading for Thawab al-a'mal))

> لَیْسَ النَّاصِبُ مَنْ نَصَبَ لَنَا أَهْلَ الْبَیْتِ لِأَنَّکَ لَمْ تَجِدْ رَجُلًا یَقُولُ أَنَا النَّاصِبُ [أُبْغِضُ] مُحَمَّداً وَ آلَ مُحَمَّدٍ وَ لَکِنَّ النَّاصِبَ مَنْ نَصَبَ لَکُمْ وَ هُوَ یَعْلَمُ أَنَّکُمْ تَتَوَالَوْنَا وَ أَنَّکُمْ مِنْ شِیعَتِنَا

The nasib is not whoever sets up [enmity] against us, the people of the house; for you will not find a man saying: 'I am the nasib [variant: I hate] Muhammad and the family of Muhammad.' But the nasib is whoever sets up [enmity] against you, while he knows that you take us as guardians (tatawallawna) and that you are of our Shi'a.

**thawab:791** (from Abu 'Abd Allah [al-Sadiq]; grade: no grade given (thaqalayn.net gives no grading for Thawab al-a'mal))

> مُدْمِنُ الْخَمْرِ کَعَابِدِ الْوَثَنِ وَ النَّاصِبُ لآِلِ مُحَمَّدٍ شَرٌّ مِنْهُ قُلْتُ جُعِلْتُ فِدَاکَ وَ مَنْ أَشَرُّ مِنْ عَابِدِ الْوَثَنِ فَقَالَ إِنَّ شَارِبَ الْخَمْرِ تُدْرِکُهُ الشَّفَاعَهُ یَوْمَ الْقِیَامَهِ وَ إِنَّ النَّاصِبَ لَوْ شَفَعَ فِیهِ أَهْلُ السَّمَاوَاتِ وَ الْأَرْضِ لَمْ یُشَفَّعُوا

The addict of wine [is] like the worshipper of an idol, and the nasib to the family of Muhammad [is] worse than him. I said: 'May I be made your ransom, and who is worse than the worshipper of an idol?' He said: 'Indeed the drinker of wine, the intercession reaches him on the Day of Resurrection; and indeed the nasib, if the people of the heavens and the earth interceded for him, they would not be granted intercession.'

- If every angel and prophet interceded, "God would never bring him out" (thawab:795, no grade given).
- For the nasib, "it does not matter [whether] he fasted or prayed" (thawab:808, no grade given).
- "Whoever claimed the Imamate and is not of its people, he is an unbeliever (kafir)" (kafi:1:372:2, unknown, not sound).

**Other bars.** Pride is barred in the Sunni wording, and then glossed as denial.

**kafi:2:310:7** (from one of the two [al-Baqir or al-Sadiq]; grade: al-Majlisi: صحيح (sound); Mirʾāt al-ʿUqūl fī Sharḥ Akhbār Āl al-Rasūl (10/208))

> لا يَدْخُلُ الْجَنَّةَ مَنْ كَانَ فِي قَلْبِهِ مِثْقَالُ حَبَّةٍ مِنْ خَرْدَلٍ مِنَ الْكِبْرِ قَالَ فَاسْتَرْجَعْتُ فَقَالَ مَا لَكَ تَسْتَرْجِعُ قُلْتُ لِمَا سَمِعْتُ مِنْكَ فَقَالَ لَيْسَ حَيْثُ تَذْهَبُ إِنَّمَا أَعْنِي الْجُحُودَ إِنَّمَا هُوَ الْجُحُودُ

He does not enter the Garden who has in his heart the weight of a grain of mustard of pride. He [the narrator] said: So I said 'To God we return' (istarja'tu). So he said: What is with you, that you say 'To God we return'? I said: For what I heard from you. So he said: It is not where you are going; I mean only denial (al-juhud); it is only denial.

*Thawab* has the same gloss: "Pride is only the denial of the truth" (thawab:856, no grade given).

**kafi:2:349:6** (from the Messenger of God (from Abu Ja'far [Muhammad al-Baqir]); grade: al-Majlisi: ضعيف (weak); Mirʾāt al-ʿUqūl fī Sharḥ Akhbār Āl al-Rasūl (10/373))

> إِيَّاكُمْ وَعُقُوقَ الْوَالِدَيْنِ فَإِنَّ رِيحَ الْجَنَّةِ تُوجَدُ مِنْ مَسِيرَةِ أَلْفِ عَامٍ وَلا يَجِدُهَا عَاقٌّ وَلا قَاطِعُ رَحِمٍ وَلا شَيْخٌ زَانٍ وَلا جَارُّ إِزَارِهِ خُيَلاءَ إِنَّمَا الْكِبْرِيَاءُ لله رَبِّ الْعَالَمِينَ

Beware of undutifulness to the parents, for the scent of the Garden is found from a journey of a thousand years, and an undutiful [one] does not find it, nor a severer of kinship, nor an old man [who is] a fornicator, nor one dragging his waist-wrapper in conceit. Grandeur (al-kibriya') belongs only to God, Lord of the worlds.

- **Talebearing.** "Forbidden is the Garden to the talebearers (al-qattatin)" (kafi:2:369:2, sound).
- **Wine.**
  - The drinker "is like one who worshipped the idols" (kafi:6:404:1, good).
  - Death within forty days of drinking is "a death of the Jahiliyya" (kafi:6:400:2, reliable).
  - The Garden is forbidden to "the addict of wine" (thawab:846, no grade given).
- **Making light of prayer.** In the Imam's last words: "our intercession does not reach whoever makes light of the prayer" (kafi:6:402:7, sound).
- **Fasting 'Ashura.** Whoever fasts it, "God gathers him with the family of Ziyad" (kafi:4:147:7, al-Majlisi silent).

#### The non-Shia, the *mustad'af* and the a'raf

The reports sort people by the Imam. Whoever knows 'Ali "is a believer", whoever denies him "is an unbeliever", and whoever is ignorant of him "is astray (dall)" (kafi:2:388:20, weak by the well-known view).
- **Who the *mustad'af* is.** They "cannot devise a stratagem to belief, and they do not disbelieve" (kafi:2:404:2, good, like sound).
- **When the class closes.** "Whoever knew the disagreement of the people is not weak (mustad'af)" (kafi:2:405:7, sound).

**kafi:2:405:5** (from Abu 'Abd Allah [Ja'far al-Sadiq]; grade: al-Majlisi: صحيح (sound); Mirʾāt al-ʿUqūl fī Sharḥ Akhbār Āl al-Rasūl (11/210))

> هُمْ أَهْلُ الْوَلايَةِ فَقُلْتُ أَيُّ وَلايَةٍ فَقَالَ أَمَا إِنَّهَا لَيْسَتْ بِالْوَلايَةِ فِي الدِّينِ وَلَكِنَّهَا الْوَلايَةُ فِي الْمُنَاكَحَةِ وَالْمُوَارَثَةِ وَالْمُخَالَطَةِ وَهُمْ لَيْسُوا بِالْمُؤْمِنِينَ وَلا بِالْكُفَّارِ وَمِنْهُمُ الْمُرْجَوْنَ لأمْرِ الله عَزَّ وَجَلَّ

They are the people of the wilaya. So I said: Which wilaya? So he said: It is not the wilaya in religion, but it is the wilaya in intermarriage and mutual inheritance and mixing. And they are not believers and not unbelievers; and of them are those deferred to the command of God.

**kafi:2:408:1** (from Abu Ja'far [Muhammad al-Baqir]; grade: al-Majlisi: موثق كالصحيح (reliable, like sound); Mirʾāt al-ʿUqūl fī Sharḥ Akhbār Āl al-Rasūl (11/216))

> وَلَكِنَّهُمْ قَوْمٌ اسْتَوَتْ حَسَنَاتُهُمْ وَسَيِّئَاتُهُمْ فَقَصُرَتْ بِهِمُ الأعْمَالُ وَإِنَّهُمْ لَكَمَا قَالَ الله عَزَّ وَجَلَّ فَقُلْتُ أَ مِنْ أَهْلِ الْجَنَّةِ هُمْ أَوْ مِنْ أَهْلِ النَّارِ فَقَالَ اتْرُكْهُمْ حَيْثُ تَرَكَهُمُ الله قُلْتُ أَ فَتُرْجِئُهُمْ قَالَ نَعَمْ أُرْجِئُهُمْ كَمَا أَرْجَأَهُمُ الله إِنْ شَاءَ أَدْخَلَهُمُ الْجَنَّةَ بِرَحْمَتِهِ وَإِنْ شَاءَ سَاقَهُمْ إِلَى النَّارِ بِذُنُوبِهِمْ وَلَمْ يَظْلِمْهُمْ فَقُلْتُ هَلْ يَدْخُلُ الْجَنَّةَ كَافِرٌ قَالَ لا قُلْتُ هَلْ يَدْخُلُ النَّارَ إِلا كَافِرٌ قَالَ فَقَالَ لا إِلا أَنْ يَشَاءَ الله

But they are a people whose good deeds and bad deeds have become equal, so the deeds fell short with them; and they are indeed as God said. So I said: Are they of the people of the Garden or of the people of the Fire? So he said: Leave them where God left them. I said: Then do you defer them (a-fa-turji'uhum)? He said: Yes, I defer them as God deferred them: if He wills, He admits them into the Garden by His mercy, and if He wills, He drives them to the Fire for their sins, and He has not wronged them. So I said: Does an unbeliever enter the Garden? He said: No. I said: Does any enter the Fire except an unbeliever? He said: So he said: No, except that God wills.

Muslims who die without the wilaya wait in the barzakh by their deeds.

**kafi:3:246:1** (from Abu Ja'far [Muhammad al-Baqir]; grade: al-Majlisi: صحيح (sound); Mirʾāt al-ʿUqūl fī Sharḥ Akhbār Āl al-Rasūl (4/228))

> قُلْتُ أَصْلَحَكَ الله فَمَا حَالُ الْمُوَحِّدِينَ الْمُقِرِّينَ بِنُبُوَّةِ مُحَمَّدٍ (صلّى اللهُ عَلَيْهِ وَآلِه) مِنَ الْمُسْلِمِينَ الْمُذْنِبِينَ الَّذِينَ يَمُوتُونَ وَلَيْسَ لَهُمْ إِمَامٌ وَلاَ يَعْرِفُونَ وَلاَيَتَكُمْ فَقَالَ أَمَّا هَؤُلاَءِ فَإِنَّهُمْ فِي حُفْرَتِهِمْ لاَ يَخْرُجُونَ مِنْهَا فَمَنْ كَانَ مِنْهُمْ لَهُ عَمَلٌ صَالِحٌ وَلَمْ يُظْهِرْ مِنْهُ عَدَاوَةً فَإِنَّهُ يُخَدُّ لَهُ خَدٌّ إِلَى الْجَنَّةِ الَّتِي خَلَقَهَا الله فِي الْمَغْرِبِ فَيَدْخُلُ عَلَيْهِ مِنْهَا الرُّوحُ فِي حُفْرَتِهِ إِلَى يَوْمِ الْقِيَامَةِ فَيَلْقَى الله فَيُحَاسِبُهُ بِحَسَنَاتِهِ وَسَيِّئَاتِهِ فَإِمَّا إِلَى الْجَنَّةِ وَإِمَّا إِلَى النَّارِ فَهَؤُلاَءِ مَوْقُوفُونَ لأَمْرِ الله قَالَ وَكَذَلِكَ يَفْعَلُ الله بِالْمُسْتَضْعَفِينَ وَالْبُلْهِ وَالأطْفَالِ وَأَوْلاَدِ الْمُسْلِمِينَ الَّذِينَ لَمْ يَبْلُغُوا الْحُلُمَ فَأَمَّا النُّصَّابُ مِنْ أَهْلِ الْقِبْلَةِ فَإِنَّهُمْ يُخَدُّ لَهُمْ خَدٌّ إِلَى النَّارِ الَّتِي خَلَقَهَا الله فِي الْمَشْرِقِ

I said: "May God set you right, then what is the state of the professors of [God's] oneness, who acknowledge the prophethood of Muhammad, from the sinning Muslims, who die and they have no Imam and do not know your wilaya?" So he said: "As for these, they are in their pit; they do not go out of it. So whoever of them had a righteous deed and did not show enmity from himself, a channel is cut for him to the garden that God created in the west, and the rest enters upon him from it in his pit until the Day of Resurrection; then he meets God, and He reckons him by his good deeds and his evil deeds, so either to the Garden or to the Fire. So these are held back for the command of God." He said: "And thus God does with the mustad'afun, and the simple-minded, and the infants, and the children of the Muslims who have not reached puberty. And as for the nasibs from the people of the qibla, a channel is cut for them to the fire that God created in the east."

**kafi:2:402:1** (from Abu 'Abd Allah [Ja'far al-Sadiq]; grade: al-Majlisi: مجهول (unknown [narrator]); Mirʾāt al-ʿUqūl fī Sharḥ Akhbār Āl al-Rasūl (11/188))

> أَ مَا رَأَيْتَ الْكَعْبَةَ وَالطَّوَافَ وَأَهْلَ الْيَمَنِ وَتَعَلُّقَهُمْ بِأَسْتَارِ الْكَعْبَةِ قُلْتُ بَلَى قَالَ أَ لَيْسَ يَشْهَدُونَ أَنْ لا إِلَهَ إِلا الله وَأَنَّ مُحَمَّداً رَسُولُ الله (صَلَّى اللهُ عَلَيْهِ وآلِه) وَيُصَلُّونَ وَيَصُومُونَ وَيَحُجُّونَ قُلْتُ بَلَى قَالَ فَيَعْرِفُونَ مَا أَنْتُمْ عَلَيْهِ قُلْتُ لا قَالَ فَمَا تَقُولُونَ فِيهِمْ قُلْتُ مَنْ لَمْ يَعْرِفْ فَهُوَ كَافِرٌ قَالَ سُبْحَانَ الله هَذَا قَوْلُ الْخَوَارِجِ ثُمَّ قَالَ إِنْ شِئْتُمْ أَخْبَرْتُكُمْ فَقُلْتُ أَنَا لا فَقَالَ أَمَا إِنَّهُ شَرٌّ عَلَيْكُمْ أَنْ تَقُولُوا بِشَيْ‏ءٍ مَا لَمْ تَسْمَعُوهُ مِنَّا

Have you not seen the Ka'ba and the circumambulation, and the people of Yemen and their clinging to the curtains of the Ka'ba? I said: Yes. He said: Do they not testify that there is no god but God and that Muhammad is the Messenger of God, and pray and fast and make the pilgrimage? I said: Yes. He said: Then do they know what you are upon? I said: No. He said: Then what do you say of them? I said: Whoever does not know is an unbeliever. He said: Glory be to God! This is the saying of the Khawarij. Then he said: If you wish, I will tell you. So I said: No. So he said: Indeed it is evil for you that you say a thing which you have not heard from us.

- **An opponent (mukhalif).** The reward of a sura is only that "Some of what he is in is lightened for him" (thawab:394, no grade given).
- **Non-Muslims.** They appear rarely:
  - At 'Arafat God forgives, "believer he was or unbeliever" (kafi:4:521:10, weak).
  - The Rum "disbelieved and did not show us enmity", unlike the people of Syria (kafi:2:410:5, good).

#### Grace and intercession

**kafi:3:242:3** (from Abu 'Abd Allah [Ja'far al-Sadiq]; grade: al-Majlisi: مجهول (unknown [narrator]); Mirʾāt al-ʿUqūl fī Sharḥ Akhbār Āl al-Rasūl (4/218))

> إِنِّي سَمِعْتُكَ وَأَنْتَ تَقُولُ كُلُّ شِيعَتِنَا فِي الْجَنَّةِ عَلَى مَا كَانَ فِيهِمْ قَالَ صَدَقْتُكَ كُلُّهُمْ وَالله فِي الْجَنَّةِ قَالَ قُلْتُ جُعِلْتُ فِدَاكَ إِنَّ الذُّنُوبَ كَثِيرَةٌ كِبَارٌ فَقَالَ أَمَّا فِي الْقِيَامَةِ فَكُلُّكُمْ فِي الْجَنَّةِ بِشَفَاعَةِ النَّبِيِّ الْمُطَاعِ أَوْ وَصِيِّ النَّبِيِّ وَلَكِنِّي وَالله أَتَخَوَّفُ عَلَيْكُمْ فِي الْبَرْزَخِ قُلْتُ وَمَا الْبَرْزَخُ قَالَ الْقَبْرُ مُنْذُ حِينِ مَوْتِهِ إِلَى يَوْمِ الْقِيَامَةِ

[The questioner said:] "I heard you saying: 'All our Shi'a are in the Garden, whatever was in them.'" He said: "I told you the truth; all of them, by God, are in the Garden." He said: I said: "May I be made your ransom, the sins are many [and] great." So he said: "As for in the Resurrection, all of you are in the Garden by the intercession of the obeyed Prophet or the executor (wasi) of the Prophet; but I, by God, fear for you in the barzakh." I said: "And what is the barzakh?" He said: "The grave, from the time of his death to the Day of Resurrection."

**kafi:2:385:7** (from Abu Ja'far [Muhammad al-Baqir]; grade: al-Majlisi: حسن كالصحيح بسنديه; Mirʾāt al-ʿUqūl fī Sharḥ Akhbār Āl al-Rasūl (11/115))

> يَدْخُلُ النَّارَ مُؤْمِنٌ قَالَ لا وَالله قُلْتُ فَمَا يَدْخُلُهَا إِلا كَافِرٌ قَالَ لا إِلا مَنْ شَاءَ الله فَلَمَّا رَدَدْتُ عَلَيْهِ مِرَاراً قَالَ لِي أَيْ زُرَارَةُ إِنِّي أَقُولُ لا وَأَقُولُ إِلا مَنْ شَاءَ الله وَأَنْتَ تَقُولُ لا وَلا تَقُولُ إِلا مَنْ شَاءَ الله

Does a believer enter the Fire? He said: No, by God. I said: Then none enters it except an unbeliever? He said: No, except whom God wills. So when I pressed him repeatedly, he said to me: O Zurara, I say 'No', and I say 'except whom God wills'; and you say 'No' and do not say 'except whom God wills'.

**kafi:2:446:10** (from the Messenger of God (from Abu 'Abd Allah [Ja'far al-Sadiq]), quoting God; grade: al-Majlisi: صحيح (sound); Mirʾāt al-ʿUqūl fī Sharḥ Akhbār Āl al-Rasūl (11/337))

> مَا مِنْ عَبْدٍ أُرِيدُ أَنْ أُدْخِلَهُ الْجَنَّةَ إِلا ابْتَلَيْتُهُ فِي جَسَدِهِ فَإِنْ كَانَ ذَلِكَ كَفَّارَةً لِذُنُوبِهِ وَإِلا شَدَّدْتُ عَلَيْهِ عِنْدَ مَوْتِهِ حَتَّى يَأْتِيَنِي وَلا ذَنْبَ لَهُ ثُمَّ أُدْخِلُهُ الْجَنَّةَ وَمَا مِنْ عَبْدٍ أُرِيدُ أَنْ أُدْخِلَهُ النَّارَ إِلا صَحَّحْتُ لَهُ جِسْمَهُ فَإِنْ كَانَ ذَلِكَ تَمَاماً لِطَلِبَتِهِ عِنْدِي وَإِلا آمَنْتُ خَوْفَهُ مِنْ سُلْطَانِهِ فَإِنْ كَانَ ذَلِكَ تَمَاماً لِطَلِبَتِهِ عِنْدِي وَإِلا وَسَّعْتُ عَلَيْهِ فِي رِزْقِهِ فَإِنْ كَانَ ذَلِكَ تَمَاماً لِطَلِبَتِهِ عِنْدِي وَإِلا هَوَّنْتُ عَلَيْهِ مَوْتَهُ حَتَّى يَأْتِيَنِي وَلا حَسَنَةَ لَهُ عِنْدِي ثُمَّ أُدْخِلُهُ النَّارَ

There is no servant whom I want to admit into the Garden except that I afflict him in his body; so if that is an expiation for his sins, [so be it,] and otherwise I make [it] hard on him at his death, until he comes to Me and there is no sin [left] for him; then I admit him into the Garden. And there is no servant whom I want to admit into the Fire except that I make his body sound for him; so if that is the completion of what he is owed (li-talibatihi) with Me, [so be it,] and otherwise I make him secure from his fear of his ruler; so if that is the completion of what he is owed with Me, and otherwise I make ample for him his provision; so if that is the completion of what he is owed with Me, and otherwise I make his death easy for him, until he comes to Me and there is no good deed [left] for him with Me; then I admit him into the Fire.

- **Mercy.** God says: "I admit him to the Garden by My mercy" (thawab:36, no grade given).
- **Exit from the Fire.**
  - A believer brings out of the Fire whoever did him a kindness, "unless he be a nasib" (kafi:2:197:6, reliable).
  - 'Ali "brings out whoever had in his heart the weight of an atom of affection (al-mawadda)" (kamil:108:11, no grade given).

#### Weeping for al-Husayn and the ziyara

These reports are almost all in *Kamil* and *Thawab*, which carry no grade.

**kamil:33:1** (from Abu 'Abd Allah [Ja'far al-Sadiq]; grade: no grade given (thaqalayn.net gives no grading for Kamil al-ziyarat))

> مَنْ أَنْشَدَ فِي الْحُسَيْنِ (ع) شِعْراً فَبَكَى وَ أَبْكَى عَشْراً كُتِبَتْ لَهُ الْجَنَّةُ وَ مَنْ أَنْشَدَ فِي الْحُسَيْنِ شِعْراً فَبَكَى وَ أَبْكَى خَمْسَةً كُتِبَتْ لَهُ الْجَنَّةُ وَ مَنْ أَنْشَدَ فِي الْحُسَيْنِ شِعْراً فَبَكَى وَ أَبْكَى وَاحِداً كُتِبَتْ لَهُمَا الْجَنَّةُ وَ مَنْ ذُكِرَ الْحُسَيْنُ (ع) عِنْدَهُ فَخَرَجَ مِنْ عَيْنِهِ [عَيْنَيْهِ] مِنَ الدُّمُوعِ مِقْدَارُ جَنَاحِ ذُبَابٍ كَانَ ثَوَابُهُ عَلَى اللَّهِ وَ لَمْ يَرْضَ لَهُ بِدُونِ الْجَنَّةِ

Whoever recites poetry on al-Husayn and weeps and makes ten weep, the Garden is written for him; and whoever recites poetry on al-Husayn and weeps and makes five weep, the Garden is written for him; and whoever recites poetry on al-Husayn and weeps and makes one weep, the Garden is written for the two of them; and whoever al-Husayn is mentioned in his presence and there comes out of his eye [variant: eyes] of tears the measure of a fly's wing, his reward was on God, and He was not content for him with less than the Garden.

- For a tear at the mention of the Imams, sins are forgiven "even if they were like the foam of the sea" (kamil:32:8).
- One report sets the tear outside measure: "For every thing [there is] a reward, except the tear for us" (kamil:33:6).

The visit is weighed against the pilgrimage, on the condition of knowing his right.

**kafi:4:580:1** (from Abu 'Abd Allah [Ja'far al-Sadiq]; grade: al-Majlisi: ضعيف (weak); Mirʾāt al-ʿUqūl fī Sharḥ Akhbār Āl al-Rasūl (8/307))

> أَيُّمَا مُؤْمِنٍ أَتَى قَبْرَ الْحُسَيْنِ (عَلَيْهِ السَّلاَم) عَارِفاً بِحَقِّهِ فِي غَيْرِ يَوْمِ عِيدٍ كَتَبَ الله لَهُ عِشْرِينَ حَجَّةً وَعِشْرِينَ عُمْرَةً مَبْرُورَاتٍ مَقْبُولاتٍ وَعِشْرِينَ حَجَّةً وَعُمْرَةً مَعَ نَبِيٍّ مُرْسَلٍ أَوْ إِمَامٍ عَدْلٍ وَمَنْ أَتَاهُ فِي يَوْمِ عِيدٍ كَتَبَ الله لَهُ مِائَةَ حَجَّةٍ وَمِائَةَ عُمْرَةٍ وَمِائَةَ غَزْوَةٍ مَعَ نَبِيٍّ مُرْسَلٍ أَوْ إِمَامٍ عَدْلٍ

Any believer who came to the grave of al-Husayn knowing his right, on other than a feast day, God writes for him twenty pilgrimages and twenty 'umras, pious [and] accepted, and twenty pilgrimages and 'umras with a sent prophet or a just Imam. And whoever came to it on a feast day, God writes for him a hundred pilgrimages, and a hundred 'umras, and a hundred raids with a sent prophet or a just Imam.

- **The condition defined.** For the visit to al-Rida, the condition is: "He knows that he is an Imam whose obedience is made obligatory" (faqih:2:1429, no grade given).
- **Precedence.** The visitors of al-Husayn "enter the Garden before the people by forty years" (kamil:53:1, no grade given).

Neglect of the visit is a bar in nine reports.

**kamil:78:5** (from Abu 'Abd Allah [Ja'far al-Sadiq]; grade: no grade given (thaqalayn.net gives no grading for Kamil al-ziyarat))

> سَأَلْتُهُ عَمَّنْ تَرَكَ الزِّيَارَةَ زِيَارَةَ قَبْرِ الْحُسَيْنِ بْنِ عَلِيٍّ مِنْ غَيْرِ عِلَّةٍ قَالَ هَذَا رَجُلٌ مِنْ أَهْلِ النَّارِ

I asked him about one who left off the visit, the visit of the grave of al-Husayn ibn 'Ali, without a cause. He said: 'This [is] a man of the people of the Fire.'

#### Jihad and the Imam

Kitab al-jihad opens with the virtue of jihad: "the swords [are] the keys of the Garden and the Fire" (kafi:5:2:1, sound). It then ties fighting to the Imam.

**kafi:5:23:3** (from Abu 'Abd Allah; grade: al-Majlisi: مجهول مرسل; Mirʾāt al-ʿUqūl fī Sharḥ Akhbār Āl al-Rasūl (18/348))

> إِنِّي رَأَيْتُ فِي الْمَنَامِ أَنِّي قُلْتُ لَكَ إِنَّ الْقِتَالَ مَعَ غَيْرِ الإمَامِ الْمَفْرُوضِ طَاعَتُهُ حَرَامٌ مِثْلَ الْمَيْتَةِ وَالدَّمِ وَلَحْمِ الْخِنْزِيرِ فَقُلْتَ لِي هُوَ كَذَلِكَ فَقَالَ أَبُو عَبْدِ اللهِ (عَلَيْهِ السَّلام) هُوَ كَذَلِكَ هُوَ كَذَلِكَ

'I saw in a dream that I said to you: Fighting with other than the Imam whose obedience is made obligatory [is] forbidden, like carrion and blood and the flesh of swine; and you said to me: It is so.' So Abu 'Abd Allah said: It is so, it is so.

**kafi:5:21:2** (from Abu al-Hasan al-Rida ['Ali ibn Musa]; grade: al-Majlisi: صحيح (sound); Mirʾāt al-ʿUqūl fī Sharḥ Akhbār Āl al-Rasūl (18/346))

> قَالَ يُرَابِطُ وَلا يُقَاتِلُ وَإِنْ خَافَ عَلَى بَيْضَةِ الإسْلامِ وَالْمُسْلِمِينَ قَاتَلَ فَيَكُونُ قِتَالُهُ لِنَفْسِهِ وَلَيْسَ لِلسُّلْطَانِ قَالَ قُلْتُ فَإِنْ جَاءَ الْعَدُوُّ إِلَى الْمَوْضِعِ الَّذِي هُوَ فِيهِ مُرَابِطٌ كَيْفَ يَصْنَعُ قَالَ يُقَاتِلُ عَنْ بَيْضَةِ الإسْلامِ لا عَنْ هَؤُلاءِ لأنَّ فِي دُرُوسِ الإسْلامِ دُرُوسَ دِينِ مُحَمَّدٍ

He said: He guards the frontier (yurabit) and does not fight; and if he fears for the core (bayda) of Islam and the Muslims, he fights, and his fighting is for himself and not for the ruler (al-sultan). He said: I said: And if the enemy comes to the place in which he is guarding, how does he act? He said: He fights in defence of the core of Islam, not in defence of these, because in the effacement of Islam [is] the effacement of the religion of Muhammad.

- **The frontier posts.** Of the caliphate's posts: "if it were good, they would not have preceded us to it" (kafi:5:19:2, unknown, not sound).
- **Raiding.** On the rule "no raiding except with a just imam", the Imam answers: "God gathers the people according to their intentions" (kafi:5:20:1, unknown, not sound).
- **Waiting for the Qa'im.** One who knows his Imam and dies before the Qa'im rises is "in the position of one who sat under his banner" (kafi:1:371:2, weak by the well-known view).

#### What the descriptions add to the Qur'an

Most descriptions repeat the Qur'an. They add the barzakh, and who is placed where.

**kafi:3:245:7** (from Abu 'Abd Allah [Ja'far al-Sadiq]; grade: al-Majlisi: موثق (reliable); Mirʾāt al-ʿUqūl fī Sharḥ Akhbār Āl al-Rasūl (4/226))

> إِنَّا نَتَحَدَّثُ عَنْ أَرْوَاحِ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ أَنَّهَا فِي حَوَاصِلِ طُيُورٍ خُضْرٍ تَرْعَى فِي الْجَنَّةِ وَتَأْوِي إِلَى قَنَادِيلَ تَحْتَ الْعَرْشِ فَقَالَ لاَ إِذاً مَا هِيَ فِي حَوَاصِلِ طَيْرٍ قُلْتُ فَأَيْنَ هِيَ قَالَ فِي رَوْضَةٍ كَهَيْئَةِ الأجْسَادِ فِي الْجَنَّةِ

[The questioner said:] "We relate about the spirits of the believers that they are in the crops of green birds grazing in the Garden and lodging in lamps beneath the Throne." So he said: "No; then they would not be [believers' spirits], in the crops of a bird." I said: "Then where are they?" He said: "In a meadow, in the form of the bodies, in the Garden."

- **Wadi al-Salam.** Every believer's spirit is told "Join Wadi al-Salam", which is "a spot of the Garden of 'Adn" (kafi:3:243:1, unknown, not sound).
- **The Prophet's station.** It is in "the Garden of 'Adn", with "these twelve of his offspring", and "no one shares it with them" (kafi:1:531:8, no grade given).
- **Karbala.** It is "set in the best meadow of the meadows of the Garden" (kamil:88:5, no grade given).

**thawab:1013** (from Abu 'Abd Allah [al-Sadiq]; grade: no grade given (thaqalayn.net gives no grading for Thawab al-a'mal))

> مَا خَلَقَ اللَّهُ خَلْقاً إِلَّا جَعَلَ لَهُ فِی الْجَنَّهِ مَنْزِلًا وَ فِی النَّارِ مَنْزِلًا فَإِذَا أُسْکِنَ أَهْلُ الْجَنَّهِ الْجَنَّهَ وَ أَهْلُ النَّارِ النَّارَ نَادَی مُنَادٍ یَا أَهْلَ الْجَنَّهِ أَشْرِفُوا فَیُشْرِفُونَ عَلَی النَّارِ وَ یُرْفَعُ لَهُمْ مَنَازِلُهُمْ فِی النَّارِ ثُمَّ یُقَالُ لَهُمْ هَذِهِ مَنَازِلُکُمُ الَّتِی لَوْ عَصَیْتُمْ رَبَّکُمْ دَخَلْتُمُوهَا

God did not create a creature but He made for it in the Garden an abode and in the Fire an abode. So when the people of the Garden are lodged in the Garden and the people of the Fire in the Fire, a caller calls: 'O people of the Garden, look down.' And they look down upon the Fire, and their abodes in the Fire are raised up for them; then it is said to them: 'These are your abodes which, had you disobeyed your Lord, you would have entered.'

#### The language of exchange

122 kept reports use it.

**kafi:2:517:1** (from Abu 'Abd Allah [Ja'far al-Sadiq]; grade: al-Majlisi: مرفوع (marfu' (chain cut, raised)); Mirʾāt al-ʿUqūl fī Sharḥ Akhbār Āl al-Rasūl (2/200))

> ثَمَنُ الْجَنَّةِ لا إِلَهَ إِلا الله وَالله أَكْبَرُ

The price of the Garden is: There is no god but God, and God is greater.

**kafi:2:176:4** (from Abu 'Abd Allah [Ja'far al-Sadiq]; grade: al-Majlisi: مجهول (unknown [narrator]); Mirʾāt al-ʿUqūl fī Sharḥ Akhbār Āl al-Rasūl (9/55))

> مَنْ زَارَ أَخَاهُ فِي الله قَالَ الله عَزَّ وَجَلَّ إِيَّايَ زُرْتَ وَثَوَابُكَ عَلَيَّ وَلَسْتُ أَرْضَى لَكَ ثَوَاباً دُونَ الْجَنَّةِ

Whoever visits his brother in God, God, Mighty and Majestic, says: Me you visited, and your reward [is] on Me, and I am not content for you with a reward below the Garden.

**kafi:8:212:1** (from Abu Ja'far [al-Baqir] (told by Abu 'Abd Allah), with a saying of Amir al-Mu'minin; grade: al-Majlisi: متلف فيه; Mirʾāt al-ʿUqūl fī Sharḥ Akhbār Āl al-Rasūl (16/132))

> وَ اعْلَمُوا أَنَّ وَلَايَتَنَا لَا تُنَالُ إِلَّا بِالْوَرَعِ وَ الِاجْتِهَادِ وَ مَنِ ائْتَمَّ مِنْكُمْ بِعَبْدٍ فَلْيَعْمَلْ بِعَمَلِهِ أَنْتُمْ شِيعَةُ اللَّهِ وَ أَنْتُمْ أَنْصَارُ اللَّهِ وَ أَنْتُمُ السَّابِقُونَ الْأَوَّلُونَ وَ السَّابِقُونَ الْآخِرُونَ وَ السَّابِقُونَ فِي الدُّنْيَا وَ السَّابِقُونَ فِي الْآخِرَةِ إِلَى الْجَنَّةِ قَدْ ضَمِنَّا لَكُمُ الْجَنَّةَ بِضَمَانِ اللَّهِ عَزَّ وَ جَلَّ وَ ضَمَانِ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ (صلى الله عليه وآله) وَ اللَّهِ مَا عَلَى دَرَجَةِ الْجَنَّةِ أَكْثَرُ أَرْوَاحاً مِنْكُمْ فَتَنَافَسُوا فِي فَضَائِلِ الدَّرَجَاتِ

And know that our wilaya is not attained except by scrupulous piety (wara') and effort; and whoever of you takes a servant as Imam, let him act by his deed. You are the Shi'a of God, and you are the helpers of God, and you are the first foremost and the last foremost, and the foremost in this world and the foremost in the hereafter to the Garden. We have guaranteed for you the Garden by the guarantee (daman) of God and the guarantee of the Messenger of God. And by God, there are not on the degree of the Garden more spirits than you; so compete in the excellences of the degrees.

- **A wage.** Of the pilgrim: "whoever made the pilgrimage for God, his reward was upon God: the Garden" (thawab:207, no grade given).
- **A market.** The pilgrimage and the 'umra are "two markets of the markets of the hereafter" (kafi:4:255:13, unknown, not sound).
- **A loan.** "The charity [is] for ten, and the loan for eighteen" (kafi:4:33:1, good or reliable).
- **A guarantee with a term.** The Prophet guarantees the Garden "on [condition] that you do not ask anyone [for] anything" (kafi:4:21:5, good).

#### Where the texts agree with the Sunni collections and where they differ

**Agreement.**
- **Pride.** Muslim 91 bars a mote of pride and defines pride as rejecting the truth and despising people. The Shia texts keep only the denial of the truth.
- **Kinship, talebearing, wine.** Severing kinship (Bukhari 5984, Muslim 2556), talebearing (Bukhari 6056, Muslim 105) and habitual wine (Nasa'i 5672, Ibn Majah 3376) are barred in both.
- **The shahada.** It forbids the Fire or brings the Garden (Bukhari 425, Muslim 33; thawab:36, no grade given).
- **Intercession.** It reaches the people of major sins (Abu Dawud 4739, Tirmidhi 2435) and the wine-drinker (thawab:791).
- **Jihad.** God guarantees or buys the Garden for the fighter in both (Bukhari 3123; kafi:5:3:4, mursal, not sound).
- **Love of 'Ali.** It is a sign of belief in both (Muslim 78; kafi:1:187:12, unknown, not sound).
- **A death of the Jahiliyya.** The Sunni texts give it for leaving the ruler or dying without a pledge of allegiance (Bukhari 7053, Muslim 1851). The Shia texts give it for not knowing the Imam.

**Difference.**
- **The five.** Bukhari 8 and Muslim 16 put the shahada first and have no wilaya.
- **The wilaya as condition.** It governs the reward of every deed and the shahada itself (kafi:2:18:5, kafi:2:520:1). The Sunni reports cited set no such condition.
- **The ruler.** The Sunni texts have jihad with every amir, righteous or wicked (Abu Dawud 2533, weak by al-Albani and Zubair Ali Zai), and patience with the ruler (Bukhari 7053). *al-Kafi* forbids fighting with other than the obligatory Imam.
- **The nasib.** No Sunni report found puts a class beyond intercession for hostility to the Prophet's household.
- **Graves and weeping.** Muslim 976 permits visiting graves to remember death, and Bukhari 1189 limits journeys to three mosques. No Sunni report found rewards weeping for al-Husayn. The Shia texts weigh his visit in pilgrimages.

### 2.13 The Sira

#### Sources and method

Three early works were swept, in the Arabic of the OpenITI corpus (fetched 2026-11-19):
- **Ibn Hisham** (d. 213/828), *al-Sira al-nabawiyya*, the recension of Ibn Ishaq (d. 150/767), in the Cairo edition of 1955, cited as volume:page;
- **al-Waqidi** (d. 207/823), *Kitab al-Maghazi*, ed. Marsden Jones, cited by its continuous pages;
- **Ibn Sa'd** (d. 230/845), *al-Tabaqat al-kubra*, in the al-Khanji edition of 2001.

Guillaume's English (*The Life of Muhammad*, 1955) was used only to check sense. His page is given for each Ibn Hisham passage.

The sweep searched the Garden words (الجنة, جنات, جنان, الفردوس), the formulas (ما لنا, موعدكم الجنة), the martyrdom words (الشهادة, شهيد, استشهد) and a few others. Of 1,584 hits, 661 were kept:
- Ibn Hisham: 139 of 305;
- al-Waqidi: 191 of 273;
- Ibn Sa'd: 331 of 1,006.

Most of the excluded hits are lists of men "killed a martyr" with no Garden stated. Reading the sections that the questions name, with a second search for suffixed plurals, found 37 further passages.

Every Arabic excerpt below was checked by machine as an exact substring of the text, and its page against the file's markers. The formulas of blessing after the names are left out of the translations. The working file is `ext-ref/religion_ends/hadith_sira.md`.

#### The pledges

**The first 'Aqaba** was "the pledge of the women", given before war was enjoined. Its terms are moral, and its reward is the Garden.

**ibnhisham:1:434** (from 'Ubada b. al-Samit, on the first 'Aqaba; also Guillaume p. 199; bukhari:18, 3892, 3893; muslim:1709)

> بايعنا رسول الله صلى الله عليه وسلم ليلة العقبة الأولى على أن لا نشرك بالله شيئا، ولا نسرق، ولا نزني، ولا نقتل أولادنا، ولا نأتي ببهتان نفتريه من بين أيدينا وأرجلنا، ولا نعصيه في معروف، فإن وفيتم فلكم الجنة، وإن غشيتم من ذلك (شيئا) فأخذتم بحده في الدنيا، فهو كفارة له، وإن سترتم عليه إلى يوم القيامة فأمركم إلى الله عز وجل، إن شاء عذب، وإن شاء غفر.

We pledged to the Messenger of God on the night of the first 'Aqaba that we would not associate anything with God, and not steal, and not commit adultery, and not kill our children, and not bring a slander we invent between our hands and our feet, and not disobey him in [what is] right. "If you fulfil, yours is the Garden. And if you commit any of that and are taken with its penalty (hadd) in this world, it is an expiation for it. And if you are concealed in it until the Day of Resurrection, your affair is to God, Mighty and Majestic: if He wills, He punishes, and if He wills, He forgives."

Bukhari 18 and Muslim 1709 have the same terms, and state the reward as due from God without naming the Garden. Bukhari 3893 names the Garden.

**The second 'Aqaba** was a pledge of war. Before it, al-'Abbas b. 'Ubada set out the cost, and the Medinans asked the price.

**ibnhisham:1:446** (from al-'Abbas b. 'Ubada b. Nadla al-Ansari, and the Khazraj, at the second 'Aqaba (from 'Asim b. 'Umar b. Qatada); also Guillaume pp. 204-205)

> إنكم تبايعونه على حرب الأحمر والأسود من الناس، فإن كنتم ترون أنكم إذا نهكت أموالكم مصيبة، وأشرافكم قتلا أسلمتموه، فمن الآن، فهو والله إن فعلتم خزي الدنيا والآخرة، وإن كنتم ترون أنكم وافون له بما دعوتموه إليه على نهكة الأموال، وقتل الأشراف، فخذوه، فهو والله خير الدنيا والآخرة، قالوا: فإنا نأخذه على مصيبة الأموال، وقتل الأشراف، فما لنا بذلك يا رسول الله إن نحن وفينا (بذلك) ؟ قال: الجنة. قالوا: ابسط يدك، فبسط يده فبايعوه. وأما عاصم بن عمر بن قتادة فقال: والله ما قال ذلك العباس إلا ليشد العقد لرسول الله صلى الله عليه وسلم في أعناقهم.

"You are pledging to him on war with the red and the black of mankind. If you think that, when a calamity wears out your wealth and killing [wears out] your nobles, you will surrender him, then [do it] from now; for it is, by God, if you do it, the disgrace of this world and the hereafter. And if you think that you will be faithful to him in what you called him to, despite the wearing out of wealth and the killing of nobles, then take him; for it is, by God, the best of this world and the hereafter." They said: "We take him on the calamity of wealth and the killing of nobles. So what is ours for that, O Messenger of God, if we fulfil (that)?" He said: "The Garden." They said: "Stretch out your hand." So he stretched out his hand, and they pledged to him. And as for 'Asim b. 'Umar b. Qatada, he said: "By God, al-'Abbas said that only to tie the knot for the Messenger of God the tighter on their necks."

Ibn Ishaq sums up the bargain on both sides.

**ibnhisham:1:454** (from Ibn Ishaq; also Guillaume p. 208)

> فلما أذن الله له فيها، وبايعهم رسول الله صلى الله عليه وسلم في العقبة الأخيرة على حرب الأحمر والأسود، أخذ لنفسه واشترط على القوم لربه، وجعل لهم على الوفاء بذلك الجنة.

When God permitted him [war], and the Messenger of God took their pledge at the last 'Aqaba on war with the red and the black, he took for himself, and stipulated upon the people for his Lord, and set for them, for fulfilment of that, the Garden.

Ibn Sa'd has the question three times, twice at 'Aqaba and once at Medina (ibnsad:4:8, 3:563, 4:343). One answer adds victory to the Garden.

**ibnsad:3:563** (from 'Ubada b. al-Walid b. 'Ubada b. al-Samit, on As'ad b. Zurara at the night of al-'Aqaba)

> إنكم تبايعونه على أن تحاربوا العرب والعجم والجن والإنس مجلبة، فقالوا: نحن حرب لمن حارب، وسلم لمن سالم، فقال أسعد بن زرارة: يا رسول الله، اشترط علي، فقال رسول الله صلى الله عليه وسلم: تبايعوني على أن تشهدوا ألا إله إلا الله، وأني رسول الله صلى الله عليه وسلم، وتقيموا الصلاة، وتؤتوا الزكاة، والسمع والطاعة، ولا تنازعوا الأمر أهله، وتمنعوني مما تمنعون منه أنفسكم وأهليكم، قالوا: نعم، قال قائل الأنصار: نعم، هذا لك يا رسول الله، فما لنا؟ قال: الجنة والنصر.

[As'ad b. Zurara said:] "You pledge to him on [condition] that you war against the Arabs and the non-Arabs, the jinn and mankind, gathered all together." They said: "We are war to whoever wars, and peace to whoever makes peace." Then As'ad b. Zurara said: "O Messenger of God, set your conditions upon me." The Messenger of God said: "You pledge to me on [condition] that you testify that there is no god but God and that I am the Messenger of God, and establish the prayer, and give the alms (zakat), and [give] hearing and obedience, and do not dispute the command with its people, and protect me from what you protect yourselves and your families from." They said: "Yes." The speaker of the Ansar said: "Yes, this is yours, O Messenger of God; so what is ours?" He said: "The Garden and victory (al-nasr)."

The phrase "the sale has profited" is not at either 'Aqaba in these three works. Ibn Sa'd uses it of Suhayb's emigration, where he paid Quraysh his wealth to be let go, and names no Garden (ibnsad:3:209).

**The pledge of Ridwan** at al-Hudaybiyya names no Garden in Ibn Hisham or Ibn Sa'd. It was "on death", or, as Jabir said, "that we would not flee" (ibnhisham:2:315). Ibn Ishaq cites Q 48:18 to 19, in which God "rewarded them with a near victory, and many spoils" (ibnhisham:2:321). Only al-Waqidi's gloss of Q 48:10 puts the Garden in place of the verse's great reward (waqidi:2:619).

#### The offer to the tribes

Ibn Hisham's section on the tribes names no reward. The Prophet asks them to "believe in me, and hold me true, and protect me" (ibnhisham:1:423). When the Banu 'Amir ask for the succession, he does not promise it.

**ibnhisham:1:424** (from Bayhara b. Firas of the Banu 'Amir b. Sa'sa'a, and the Messenger of God (from al-Zuhri); also Guillaume p. 195)

> أرأيت إن نحن بايعناك على أمرك، ثم أظهرك الله على من خالفك، أيكون لنا الأمر من بعدك؟ قال: الأمر إلى الله يضعه حيث يشاء، قال: فقال له: أفتهدف نحورنا للعرب دونك، فإذا أظهرك الله كان الأمر لغيرنا! لا حاجة لنا بأمرك، فأبوا عليه.

"Do you see, if we pledge to you on your affair, then God makes you prevail over whoever opposed you, will the command (al-amr) be ours after you?" He said: "The command is God's; He puts it where He wills." He said to him: "Are our throats then to be made a target for the Arabs on your behalf, and when God makes you prevail, the command is for others? We have no need of your affair." So they refused him.

To Quraysh at Abu Talib's deathbed he offers "one word" by which "you will rule the Arabs" (ibnhisham:1:417). The Garden enters the offer in Abu Jahl's report of it, which the Prophet affirms.

**ibnhisham:1:483** (from Abu Jahl, at the door of the Messenger of God on the night of the hijra; Ibn Ishaq; also Guillaume p. 222)

> إن محمدا يزعم أنكم إن تابعتموه على أمره، كنتم ملوك العرب والعجم، ثم بعثتم من بعد موتكم، فجعلت لكم جنان كجنان الأردن، وإن لم تفعلوا كان له فيكم ذبح، ثم بعثتم من بعد موتكم، ثم جعلت لكم نار تحرقون فيها. قال: وخرج عليهم رسول الله صلى الله عليه وسلم، فأخذ حفنة من تراب في يده، ثم قال أنا أقول ذلك، أنت أحدهم.

"Muhammad claims that if you follow him in his affair, you will be kings of the Arabs and the non-Arabs; then you will be raised after your death, and gardens like the gardens of the Jordan will be made for you. And if you do not, there will be slaughter for him among you; then you will be raised after your death, then a fire will be made for you in which you are burned." He said: And the Messenger of God came out to them, and took a handful of dust in his hand, then said: "I say that. You are one of them."

Ibn Sa'd joins both rewards in the offer at the fairs.

**ibnsad:1:184** (from al-Waqidi's composite report ("they said": 'Abd Allah b. Ka'b b. Malik, al-Zuhri, 'Asim b. 'Umar b. Qatada, Yazid b. Ruman and others))

> يوافي المواسم كل عام يتبع الحاج في منازلهم في المواسم بعكاظ ومجنة وذي المجاز يدعوهم إلى أن يمنعوه حتى يبلغ رسالات ربه ولهم الجنة، فلا يجد أحدا ينصره ولا يجيبه، حتى إنه ليسأل عن القبائل ومنازلها قبيلة قبيلة، ويقول: يا أيها الناس، قولوا: لا إله إلا الله تفلحوا وتملكوا بها العرب، وتذل لكم العجم، وإذا آمنتم كنتم ملوكا في الجنة

He would come to the fairs every year, following the pilgrims in their camps at the fairs, at 'Ukaz and Majanna and Dhu al-Majaz, calling them to protect him until he conveyed the messages of his Lord, and theirs [would be] the Garden. And he did not find anyone to help him or answer him, so that he would ask about the tribes and their camps, tribe by tribe, and say: "O people, say: There is no god but God, [and] you will prosper, and by it you will rule the Arabs, and the non-Arabs will be humbled to you; and if you believed, you would be kings in the Garden."

#### The battles

**Badr.** The promise is the Garden for the one killed steadfast and advancing.

**ibnhisham:1:627** (from the Messenger of God at Badr, and 'Umayr b. al-Humam; also Guillaume p. 300; muslim:1901)

> والذي نفس محمد بيده، لا يقاتلهم اليوم رجل فيقتل صابرا محتسبا، مقبلا غير مدبر، إلا أدخله الله الجنة. فقال عمير بن الحمام، أخو بني سلمة، وفي يده تمرات يأكلهن: بخ بخ ، أفما بيني وبين أن أدخل الجنة إلا أن يقتلني هؤلاء، ثم قذف التمرات من يده وأخذ سيفه، فقاتل القوم حتى قتل.
